produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that was held on November 19, 2016 at [[Odyssey (Belfast)|The SSE Arena]] in [[Belfast]], [[Northern Ireland]]{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Kim-and-Nelson-to-Headline-UFC-Return-to-Belfast|title=Kim and Nelson to headline UFC's return to Belfast|publisher=ufc.com|author=Press Release|date=1 September 2016|access-date=1 September 2016}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the second that the organisation hosted in [[Northern Ireland]], with the first being [[UFC 72]] in June 2007.\n\nA welterweight bout between ranked contenders [[Dong Hyun Kim]] and [[Gunnar Nelson (fighter)|Gunnar Nelson]] was expected to serve as the headlined of the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/8/24/12635044/gunnar-nelson-vs-dong-hyun-kim-slated-for-ufcs-return-to-northern|title=Gunnar Nelson vs. Dong Hyun Kim slated for UFC's return to Northern Ireland|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=24 August 2016|access-date=24 August 2016}} However, on 21 October, it was announced that Nelson pulled out due to an injury and the fight was cancelled. Kim was rescheduled to compete at [[UFC 207]].{{cite web|url=http://www.sportsjoe.ie/mma/ufc-belfast-loses-main-event-as-sbgs-gunnar-nelson-suffers-injury/99909|title=UFC Belfast loses main event as SBG's Gunnar Nelson suffers injury|publisher=sportsjoe.ie|author=Darragh Murphy|date=21 October 2016|access-date=21 October 2016|archive-date=23 October 2016|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20161023230408/http://www.sportsjoe.ie/mma/ufc-belfast-loses-main-event-as-sbgs-gunnar-nelson-suffers-injury/99909|url-status=dead}} In turn, a middleweight rematch between former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Light Heavyweight Championship|Strikeforce Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Gegard Mousasi]] and [[Uriah Hall]] was announced as the new headliner.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/10/21/13363116/gegard-mousasi-vs-uriah-hall-2-to-main-event-ufc-belfast-with-gunnar|title=Gegard Mousasi vs. Uriah Hall 2 to main event UFC Belfast with Gunnar Nelson out|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=21 October 2016|access-date=21 October 2016}} The pairing met previously in September 2015 at [[UFC Fight Night: Barnett vs. Nelson]] with Hall taking the victory via second round highlight reel TKO in what was considered a huge upset.{{cite web|url=http://ftw.usatoday.com/2015/09/ufc-japan-uriah-hall-spinning-kick|title=UFC's Uriah Hall stuns Gegard Mousasi with a gorgeous spinning back kick|author=Nick Schwartz|publisher=USA Today Sports|date=27 September 2015|access-date=21 October 2016}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Ross Pearson]] and [[James Krause (fighter)|James Krause]] was originally booked for [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Cláudia#The Ultimate Fighter 23 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Cláudia Finale]]. However, Krause pulled out due to undisclosed reasons.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/06/james-krause-withdraws-from-the-ultimate-fighter-23-finale-ufc-seeks-new-opponent-for-ross-pearson|title=James Krause withdraws from The Ultimate Fighter 23 Finale, UFC seeks new opponent for Ross Pearson|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=13 June 2016|access-date=10 October 2016}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event and was expected to serve as the co-main event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/ross-pearson-vs-james-krause-slated-for-ufc-fight-night-99-in-belfast|title=Ross Pearson vs. James Krause slated for UFC Fight Night 99 in Belfast|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=22 September 2016|access-date=10 October 2016}} Subsequently, on 26 October, Krause pulled out of the fight citing a torn hamstring.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/10/james-krause-injured-out-of-ufc-fight-night-99-co-main-event-vs-ross-pearson|title=James Krause injured, out of UFC Fight Night 99 co-main event vs. Ross Pearson|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=26 October 2016|access-date=26 October 2016}} He was then replaced by [[Stevie Ray (MMA)|Stevie Ray]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/10/26/13417990/stevie-ray-expected-to-replace-injured-james-krause-against-ross|title=Stevie Ray expected to replace injured James Krause against Ross Pearson at UFN: Belfast|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=26 October 2016|access-date=26 October 2016}}\n\nAs a result of the cancellation of [[UFC Fight Night: Lamas vs. Penn]], a flyweight bout between [[Kyoji Horiguchi]] and [[Ali Bagautinov]] was rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.sportsjoe.ie/mma/ufc-belfast-benefits-greatly-cancelled-manila-event-exciting-flyweight-bout-finds-new-home/98867|title=UFC Belfast benefits greatly from cancelled Manila event as exciting flyweight bout finds new home|publisher=sportsjoe.ie|author=Ben Kiely|date=13 October 2016|access-date=13 October 2016|archive-date=14 October 2016|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20161014063725/http://www.sportsjoe.ie/mma/ufc-belfast-benefits-greatly-cancelled-manila-event-exciting-flyweight-bout-finds-new-home/98867|url-status=dead}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Zak Cummings]] came in at 172.8 lb, almost two pounds over the welterweight limit of 171 lb. As a result, Cummings was fined 20% of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Alexander Yakovlev (fighter)|Alexander Yakovlev]] and the bout proceeded as scheduled at a catchweight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/ufc-fight-night-99-early-weigh-in-results-and-live-video-stream-4-a-m-et-1|title=UFC Fight Night 99 weigh-in results: Mousasi-Hall official, McCall-Seery scrapped|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=18 November 2016|access-date=18 November 2016}}\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Ian McCall (fighter)|Ian McCall]] and [[Neil Seery]] was scheduled for the event. However, as Seery was weighing in for the event, it was announced that McCall had to pull out of the fight after becoming ill due to the effects of his [[weight cutting|weight cut]] and the bout was scrapped.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/again-seriously-illness-forces-ian-mccall-out-of-ufc-fight-night-99-fight-with-neil-seery|title=Again? Seriously? in Northern Ireland sport]]\n[[Category:November 2016 sports events in the United Kingdom|UFC Fight Night: Mousasi vs Hall 2]]"}}}],"langlinks":[{"lang":"ja","title":"UFC Fight Night: Mousasi vs. Hall 2"}]},{"pageid":51564404,"ns":0,"title":"UFC 206","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2016}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC 206: Holloway vs. Pettis\n|image=UFC_206_poster.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2016|12|10}}\n|venue=[[Air Canada Centre]]\n|city=[[Toronto, Ontario]], [[Canada]]\n|attendance= 18,057{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/ufc-206-attendance-event-nears-highest-attendance-in-ufcs-air-canada-centre-history-1|title=UFC 206 attendance: Event nears highest attendance in UFC's Air Canada Centre history|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=December 11, 2016}}\n|gate= $1,843,101\n|buyrate = \n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Abdurakhimov]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC on Fox: VanZant vs. Waterson]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 206: Holloway vs. Pettis''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC) held on December 10, 2016, at the [[Air Canada Centre]] in [[Toronto]], Ontario, Canada.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/8/19/12562718/ufc-releases-schedule-for-rest-of-2016-including-ufc-206-in-toronto|title=UFC releases schedule for rest of 2016 including UFC 206 in Toronto|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-08-19|access-date=2016-08-19}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] rematch between the former champion [[Daniel Cormier]] and [[Anthony Johnson (fighter)|Anthony Johnson]] was originally expected to headline the event.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/10/5/13179942/daniel-cormier-vs-anthony-johnson-rematch-set-for-ufc-206|title=Daniel Cormier vs. Anthony Johnson rematch set for UFC 206|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2016-10-05|access-date=2016-10-05}} The pairing met previously in May 2015 at [[UFC 187]] with Cormier winning the fight (and the vacant title) via submission in the third round.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/05/ufc-187-results-daniel-cormier-wears-down-anthony-johnson-for-third-round-tap|title=UFC 187 results: Daniel Cormier wears down Anthony Johnson for third-round tap|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2015-05-24|access-date=2016-10-05}} However, on November 25, Cormier pulled out due to injury and the bout was scrapped.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/report-daniel-cormier-injured-forced-out-of-ufc-206-headliner-vs-anthony-johnson|title=Report: Daniel Cormier injured, forced out of UFC 206 headliner vs. Anthony Johnson|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-11-25|access-date=2016-11-25}} Promotion officials reportedly tried to book Johnson against former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Light Heavyweight Championship|Strikeforce Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Gegard Mousasi]], but the former opted to wait for Cormier to return. The bout was rescheduled for [[UFC 210]]. Therefore, a featherweight bout between [[Max Holloway]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Anthony Pettis]] was shifted to the headlining spot. Another component was added to the fight, as it was expected to be for the interim [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] status.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/11/26/13750428/max-holloway-vs-anthony-pettis-for-interim-title-new-ufc-206-main|title=Max Holloway vs. Anthony Pettis for interim title now slated for UFC 206 main event|author= Ariel Helwani|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2016-11-26|access-date=2016-11-26}} The current champion (as well as lightweight champion) [[Conor McGregor]] vacated his title and former champion/current interim titleholder [[José Aldo]] became the undisputed champion.{{cite web |url=http://www.ufc.com/news/McGregor-vacates-featherweight-title|title=McGregor vacates featherweight title; Aldo new undisputed champion|author=Staff|publisher=ufc.com|date=2016-11-26|access-date=2016-11-26}}{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/stripping-conor-mcgregor-of-his-ufc-featherweight-title-makes-some-sense-but-not-like-this|title=Stripping Conor McGregor of his UFC featherweight title makes sense – but not like this|author=Ben Fowlkes|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-11-27|access-date=2016-11-27}}\n\nPromotional newcomer [[Poliana Botelho]] was expected to face former [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] challenger [[Valérie Létourneau]] at the event. However, Botelho was removed from the fight for undisclosed reasons on October 26 and was replaced by fellow newcomer [[Viviane Pereira]].{{cite web |url=http://www.rds.ca/combat/ufc/amm-la-quebecoise-valerie-letourneau-affrontera-finalement-viviane-pereira-lors-d-ufc-206-1.3600466|title=Valérie Létourneau finally confronts Viviane Pereira at UFC 206|author=Staff|publisher=rds.ca|date=2016-10-26|access-date=2016-10-26}} {{in lang|fr}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[John Moraga]] was expected to face former [[List of Bellator champions#Bantamweight Championship|Bellator Bantamweight Champion]] [[Zach Makovsky]] at the event. However, Moraga pulled out of the fight in early November citing injury and was replaced by [[Dustin Ortiz]].{{cite news|url=https://www.tsn.ca/ortiz-replaces-moraga-on-ufc-206-card-1.602364|title=Ortiz replaces Moraga on UFC 206 card|publisher=tsn.ca|author=Staff|date=2016-11-07|access-date=2016-11-07}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Tim Kennedy (fighter)|Tim Kennedy]] and former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] and divisional newcomer [[Rashad Evans]] was originally booked for [[UFC 205]]. However, Evans was pulled from the fight days before the event after an undisclosed irregularity was found during his pre-fight medical exam. Subsequently, Kennedy was removed from the card as well.{{cite web|url=http://fansided.com/2016/11/08/rashad-evans-off-ufc-205/|title=Rashad Evans pulled from UFC 205 fight against Tim Kennedy|publisher=fansided.com|author=Jose Youngs|date=2016-11-08|access-date=2016-11-08}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/11/8/13567552/rashad-evans-vs-tim-kennedy-fight-scratched-from-ufc-205|title=Rashad Evans vs. Tim Kennedy fight scratched from UFC 205|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-11-08|access-date=2016-11-08}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/rashad-evans-vs-tim-kennedy-rescheduled-for-ufc-206-in-toronto|title=Rashad Evans vs. Tim Kennedy rescheduled for UFC 206 in Toronto|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2016-11-09|access-date=2016-11-09}} Subsequently, Evans once again was unable to obtain medical clearance to compete on the card and was pulled from the bout on November 21. An irregularity in his pre-fight [[Magnetic resonance imaging|MRI]] was responsible for both removals and he is expected to undergo an additional screening with a neurologist to prove he is healthy enough to fight, before possibly returning to the cage in January or February, according to his manager.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/manager-rashad-evans-not-cleared-to-fight-at-ufc-206|title=Manager: Rashad Evans not cleared to fight at UFC 206|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2016-11-21|access-date=2016-11-21}} [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] middleweight winner [[Kelvin Gastelum]] was announced as his replacement on November 26, pending commission approval due to his suspension after [[UFC 205]].{{cite web |url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Pending-Commission-Approval-Tim-Kennedy-vs-Kelvin-Gastelum-Set-for-UFC-206-114101|title=Pending commission approval, Tim Kennedy vs. Kelvin Gastelum set for UFC 206|author=Tristen Critchfield|publisher=sherdog.com|date=2016-11-26|access-date=2016-11-26}} He was granted his liberation 4 days later.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/11/29/13788382/kelvin-gastelum-free-of-suspension-will-face-tim-kennedy-at-ufc-206|title=Kelvin Gastelum free of suspension, will face Tim Kennedy at UFC 206|author=Marc Raimondi|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2016-11-29|access-date=2016-11-29}}\n\nA welterweight bout between [[Chad Laprise]] and [[Li Jingliang]] was expected to take place at the event. However, Laprise pulled out of the fight on November 16 citing an undisclosed injury. As a result, Li was pulled from the card entirely and will be rescheduled at a future event.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.ca/news/Laprise-Injured-Li-Bout-Off|title=Laprised injured, Li bout off|publisher=ufc.ca.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-11-16|access-date=2016-11-16}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Pettis came in at 148 lb, three pounds over the featherweight limit of 145 lb. for a championship fight. As a result, in the event that Pettis were to win the fight with Holloway, he would be ineligible for the championship, while Holloway was still eligible.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/anthony-pettis-misses-weight-only-max-holloway-eligible-for-title-in-ufc-206-headliner|title=Anthony Pettis misses weight, only Max Holloway eligible for title in UFC 206|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn and Dann Stupp|date=2016-12-09|access-date=2016-12-09}} Pettis was fined 20% of his purse, which will go Holloway and the bout proceeded as scheduled at a catchweight. Pettis was the first fighter to miss weight for a UFC championship bout since [[Travis Lutter]] at [[UFC 67]] in February 2007.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/12/9/13898766/anthony-pettis-misses-weight-for-his-ufc-206-interim-title-fight-with|title=Anthony Pettis misses weight for his UFC 206 interim title fight with Max Holloway|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Chuck Mindenhall|date=2016-12-09|access-date=2016-12-09}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/ufc-206-early-weigh-in-results-and-live-video-stream-9-a-m-et-1-1-1|title=UFC 206 early weigh-in results: 3, including headliner Anthony Pettis, miss weight|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-09|access-date=2016-12-09}} [[Rustam Khabilov]] and [[Valérie Létourneau]] also missed the required weight for their respective fights. They were both fined 20% of their purse which will go to their respective opponents, [[Jason Saggo]] and Viviane Pereira. The bouts proceeded at catchweight.\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Catchweight (148 lb)\n|[[Max Holloway]]\n|def.\n|[[Anthony Pettis]]\n|TKO (body kick $50,000 bonuses:{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/ufc-206-bonuses-do-you-really-have-to-ask-if-cub-swanson-dooho-choi-was-fight-of-the-night|title=UFC 206 bonuses: Do you really have to ask Choi'''\n*'''Performance of the Night:''' '''Max Holloway''' and '''Lando Vannata'''\n\n==Swanson vs. Choi==\nAfter receiving fight of the year in 2016{{cite news |last1=Okamoto |first1=Brett |title=2016 ESPN.com fight of the year: Cub Swanson vs. Doo Ho Choi |url=https://www.espn.co.uk/mma/story/_/id/18400151/cub-swanson-vs-doo-ho-choi-ufc-mma |access-date=29 June 2022 |publisher=ESPN |date=3 January 2017}} it was announced by the UFC on April 10, 2022 that Cub Swanson vs. Doo Ho Choi would be inducted into the [[UFC Hall of Fame]] in 2022.{{cite news |last1=Smith |first1=Brian |title=Cub Swanson vs Dooho Choi To Be Inducted Into 2022 UFC Hall Of Fame |url=https://www.ufc.com/news/cub-swanson-vs-dooho-choi-be-inducted-2022-ufc-hall-fame |access-date=29 June 2022 Brunson","revisions":[{"slots":{"main":{"contentmodel":"wikitext","contentformat":"text/x-wiki","content":"{{short description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2016}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC Fight Night: Whittaker vs. Brunson\n|image=Rockhold x Jacare 2.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2016|11|27}}\n|venue=[[Rod Laver Arena]]\n|city=[[Melbourne]], Australia\n|attendance=13,721{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/ufc-fight-night-101-draws-announced-sell-out-of-13721-for-2-2-million-aud-live-gate|title=UFC Fight Night 101 draws announced sell-out of 13,721 for $2.2 million AUD live gate|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-11-27|access-date=2016-11-27}}\n|gate = 2,200,000 [[AUD]]\n|buyrate=\n|previousevent=[[UFC Fight Night: Bader vs. Nogueira 2]]\n|followingevent=[[The Ultimate Fighter: Tournament of Champions#The Ultimate Fighter 24 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Tournament of Champions Finale]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Whittaker vs. Brunson''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 101''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on November 27, 2016, at [[Rod Laver Arena]] in [[Melbourne]], Australia.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/luke-rockhold-and-jacare-souza-set-to-rematch-at-ufc-fight-night-101|title=Luke Rockhold and 'Jacare' Souza set to rematch at UFC Fight Night 101|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-09-11|access-date=2016-09-11}}{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Rockhold-Souza-II-Headlines-Down-Under-in-November-091116|title=Rockhold-Souza II headlines \"Down Under\" in November|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-09-11|access-date=2016-09-11}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was the second that the organization hosted in [[Melbourne]], with the first being [[UFC 193]] in November 2015.\n\nThe event was expected to be headlined by a middleweight rematch between former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Middleweight Championship|Strikeforce]] and [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Luke Rockhold]] and former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Middleweight Championship|Strikeforce Middleweight Champion]] [[Ronaldo Souza]]. The duo first met in September 2011 at [[Strikeforce: Barnett vs. Kharitonov]], in which Rockhold won a unanimous decision to capture Souza's title.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2013/4/28/4276470/history-in-the-making-luke-rockhold-jacare-souza-strikeforce-championship-mma|title=History in the making: Luke Rockhold upsets 'Jacare' Souza to win Strikeforce middleweight title|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=2013-04-28|access-date=2016-09-11}} However, on November 1, Rockhold was ruled out of the contest due to a sprained [[Anterior cruciate ligament|ACL]]. Subsequently, Souza was removed from the card entirely and is expected to be rescheduled for another event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/11/1/13491328/luke-rockhold-vs-ronaldo-jacare-souza-rematch-canceled|title=Luke Rockhold vs. Ronaldo \"Jacaré\" Souza rematch canceled|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-11-01|access-date=2016-11-01}} In turn, a middleweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: The Smashes]] welterweight winner [[Robert Whittaker (fighter)|Robert Whittaker]] and [[Derek Brunson]] was elevated to serve as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/11/2/13497524/robert-whittaker-vs-derek-brunson-promoted-to-main-event-of-ufc-fight|title=Robert Whittaker vs. Derek Brunson promoted to main event of UFC Fight Night 101 in Australia|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2016-11-02|access-date=2016-11-02}}\n\n[[File:Rod Laver Arena (8985219166).jpg|thumb|left|240px|[[Rod Laver Arena]] is widely known as the venue which hosts the annual [[Australian Open]] in [[tennis]].]]\n\nAs a result of the cancellation of [[UFC Fight Night: Lamas vs. Penn]], bouts between [[Yao Zhikui]] vs. [[Jenel Condit]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/8/9/12416676/ufc-s-ning-guangyou-fails-drug-test-for-clenbuterol-usada-finds-no|title=UFC's Ning Guangyou fails drug test for clenbuterol, USADA finds no fault|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-08-09|access-date=2016-11-15}} In turn, the bout was postponed again due to alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues for Ning, which restricted the timing of his travel.{{cite web|url=http://uk.ufc.com/news/Guangyou-Vera-off-vancouver|title=Guangyou-Vera bout off Vancouver card|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-08-22|access-date=2016-11-15}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=http://mma.uno/chito-vera-enfrentara-ning-guangyou-ufc-fight-night-101/|title=Chito Vera Night: Bader vs. Nogueira 2\n|image=UFC SP BRA poster.jpeg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2016|11|19}}\n|venue=[[Ginásio do Ibirapuera]]\n|city=[[São Paulo]], [[Brazil]]\n|attendance=9,028{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/ufc-fight-night-100-draws-announced-attendance-of-9028|title=UFC Fight Night 100 draws announced attendance of Brunson]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Bader vs. Nogueira 2''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 100''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on November 19, 2016, at [[Ginásio do Ibirapuera]] in [[São Paulo]], [[Brazil]]{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/9/20/12990776/alexander-gustafsson-vs-antonio-rogerio-nogueira-slated-for-ufc-fight|title=Alexander Gustafsson vs. Antonio Rogerio Nogueira slated for UFC Fight Night 100|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-09-20|access-date=2016-09-20}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was initially expected to be headlined by a light heavyweight bout between former two-time [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Alexander Gustafsson]] and [[Antônio Rogério Nogueira]] The promotion had previously scheduled the pairing as main event on two other occasions. First in April 2012 at [[UFC on Fuel TV: Gustafsson vs. Silva]] and again in March 2014 at [[UFC Fight Night: Gustafsson vs. Manuwa]]. In both instances Nogueira pulled out due to injury.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmamania.com/2012/3/6/2849882/ufc-on-fuel-tv-2-thiago-silva-vs-alexander-gustafsson-booked-with|title=UFC on FUEL TV 2: Thiago Silva vs Alexander Gustafsson booked with Antonio Rogerio Nogueira injured | publisher=mmamania.com|author=Gene Mrosko|date=2012-03-06|access-date=2016-09-20}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2013/10/31/5052980/antonio-rogerio-nogueira-injured-out-of-alexander-gustafsson-fight|title=Antonio Rogerio Nogueira injured, out of Alexander Gustafsson fight|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2013-10-31|access-date=2016-09-20}} However on September 30, Gustafsson pulled out of the bout citing an injury of his own. In turn, he was replaced by [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Nogueira vs. Team Mir]] light heavyweight winner [[Ryan Bader]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/9/30/13127398/alexander-gustafsson-out-ryan-bader-in-vs-rogerio-nogueira-at-ufc|title=Alexander Gustafsson out, Ryan Bader in vs. Rogerio Nogueira at UFC Fight Night 100|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-09-30|access-date=2016-09-30}} Bader defeated Nogueira in their previous encounter six years prior at [[UFC 119]] by unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2010/9/26/1712359/ufc-119-results-ryan-bader-takes-down-antonio-rogerio-nogueira|title=UFC 119 results: Ryan Bader takes down Antônio Rogério Nogueira|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Nate Wilcox|date=2016-09-26|access-date=2016-10-01|archive-date=2017-02-24|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170224023036/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2010/9/26/1712359/ufc-119-results-ryan-bader-takes-down-antonio-rogerio-nogueira|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Michael Graves (fighter)|Michael Graves]] was scheduled to face [[Sérgio Moraes]] on the card. However, Graves was removed from the fight on October 3 after he was arrested on a misdemeanor [[Battery (crime)|battery]] charge.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/10/ufc-welterweight-michael-graves-removed-from-ufc-fight-night-100-bout-after-arrest-for-battery|title=UFC welterweight Michael Graves removed from UFC Fight Night 100 bout after arrest for battery|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-10-03|access-date=2016-10-03}} He was replaced by [[Zak Ottow]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/10/zak-ottow-replaces-michael-graves-against-sergio-moraes-at-ufc-fight-night-100|title=Zak Ottow replaces Michael Graves against Sergio Moraes at UFC Fight Night 100|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-10-04|access-date=2016-10-04}}\n\n[[Matheus Nicolau]] was expected to face [[Ulka Sasaki]] at the event. However, on November 3, Nicolau was pulled from the bout after [[USADA]] revealed a potential anti-doping violation from a sample taken October 13. In turn, Sasaki was pulled from the event entirely and was later rescheduled to face [[Wilson Reis]] on February 12, 2017 at [[UFC 208]]{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/flyweight-matheus-nicolau-pulled-from-ufc-fight-night-100-for-potential-anti-doping-violation|title=Flyweight Matheus Nicolau pulled from UFC Fight Night 100 for potential anti-doping violation|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2016-11-03|date=2016-11-03}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent American English|date=September 2026}}\n{{Use mdy dates|date=September 2026}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Abdurakhimov\n|image=UFC_Albany_poster.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2016|12|09}}\n|venue=[[Times Union Center]]\n|city=[[Albany, New York|Albany]], [[New York (state)|New York]]\n|attendance=6,216{{cite Ultimate Fighter: Tournament of Champions Finale]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC 206|UFC 206: Holloway vs. Pettis]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Abdurakhimov''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 102''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event held by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] on December 9, 2016, at [[Times Union Center]] in [[Albany, New York|Albany]], [[New York (state)|New York]]{{cite web|url=http://www.timesunion.com/local/article/UFC-is-coming-to-Times-Union-Center-9173691.php|title=UFC is coming to Times Union Center|publisher=timesunion.com|author=Rick Karlin|date=2016-08-19|access-date=2016-08-19}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event was the first that the organization hosted in Albany.\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Derrick Lewis]] and [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]] served as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/10/ufc-fight-night-102-gets-eight-new-fights-including-derrick-lewis-vs-shamil-abdurakhimov-headliner-1|title=UFC Fight Night 102 gets eight new fights, including Derrick Lewis vs. Shamil Abdurakhimov headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-10-14|access-date=2016-10-14}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Josh Samman]] and [[Oluwale Bamgbose]] was briefly linked to the event. However the pairing never materialized as Samman died on October 5, 2016.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/10/5/13175024/ufc-fighter-josh-samman-dead-at-the-age-of-28|title=UFC fighter Josh Samman dead at the age of 28|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-10-05|access-date=2016-10-05}} Joe Gigliotti was tabbed as his replacement. In turn, Bamgbose pulled out of the fight in mid-November citing an injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Gerald Meerschaert]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmamad.com/2016/11/16/gerald-meerschaert-replaces-oluwale-bamgbose-faces-joe-gigliotti-at-ufc-albany/|archive-url=https://archive.today/20161213072135/http://www.mmamad.com/2016/11/16/gerald-meerschaert-replaces-oluwale-bamgbose-faces-joe-gigliotti-at-ufc-albany/|url-status=usurped|archive-date=December 13, 2016|title=Gerald Meerschaert replaces Oluwale Bamgbose; faces Joe Gigliotti at UFC Albany|publisher=mmamad.com|author=Dale Jordan|date=2016-11-16|access-date=2016-11-16}}\n\nPromotional newcomer [[Charlie Ward (fighter)|Charlie Ward]] was expected to face [[Randy Brown (fighter)|Randy Brown]] at the event. However, Ward was pulled from the event in early November due to alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues which restricted his travel and was replaced by fellow newcomer [[Brian Camozzi]].{{cite web|url=https://www.newsday.com/sports/mixed-martial-arts/randy-brown-gets-new-opponent-for-ufc-fight-night-in-albany-c60546|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20161108200858/http://www.newsday.com/sports/mixed-martial-arts/randy-brown-gets-new-opponent-for-ufc-fight-night-in-albany-1.12572442|url-status=live|archive-date=November 8, 2016|title=Randy Brown gets new opponent for UFC Fight Night in Albany|publisher=newsday.com|author=Mark La Monica|date=2016-11-07|access-date=2016-11-07}}\n\n[[Zubaira Tukhugov]] was scheduled to face [[Tiago Trator]] at the event. However, on November 14, Tukhugov was pulled from the card after being notified by [[USADA]] of a potential anti-doping violation stemming from an out-of-competition sample collected earlier.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-statement-on-Zubaira-Tukhugov--111416|title=UFC statement on Zubaira Tukhugov|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2016-11-14|access-date=2016-11-14}} On November 28, Tukhugov was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Shane Burgos]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/11/28/13767868/undefeated-prospect-shane-burgos-signs-with-the-ufc-meets-tiago|title=Trator gets new opponent at UFC Fight Night 102|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-11-28|access-date=2016-11-28}}\n\n[[Aljamain Sterling]] was expected to face [[Raphael Assunção]] at the event. However, Sterling pulled out of the fight on November 23 citing injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/aljamain-sterling-out-at-ufc-fight-night-102-promotion-needs-u-s-based-foe-for-raphael-assuncao-2|title=Aljamain Sterling out at UFC Fight Night 102, promotion needs U.S.-based foe for Raphael Assuncao|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2016-11-24|access-date=2016-11-24}} Assunção was subsequently removed from the card with the pairing expected to be rescheduled for a future event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/11/28/13769620/raphael-assuncao-removed-from-ufc-albany-card-after-aljamain-sterling|title=Raphael Assuncao removed from UFC Albany card after Aljamain Sterling injury|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-11-28|access-date=2016-11-28}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/off-ufc-fight-night-102-card-aljamain-sterling-vs-raphael-assuncao-targeted-for-future-event|title=Off UFC Fight Night 102 card, Aljamain Sterling vs. Raphael Assuncao targeted for future event|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-11-29|access-date=2016-11-29}}\n\n[[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Cláudia]] women's strawweight winner [[Tatiana Suarez]] was expected to face [[Juliana Lima]] at the event. However, Suarez pulled out of the fight on November 23 citing injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/tatiana-suarez-injured-out-of-ufc-fight-night-102-fight-with-juliana-lima|title=Tatiana Suarez injured, out of UFC Fight Night 102 fight with Juliana Lima|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-11-24|access-date=2016-11-24}} She was replaced on December 2 by promotional newcomer [[JJ Aldrich]].{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Safarov-and-Aldrich-to-Make-Debuts-in-Albany|title=Safarov and Aldrich to make debuts in Albany|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-12-02|access-date=2016-12-02}}\n\n[[Patrick Cummins (fighter)|Patrick Cummins]] was expected to face [[Gian Villante]] at the event. However, Cummins pulled out of the fight on December 2 citing a [[staph infection]].{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/12/2/13820928/patrick-cummins-out-ufc-fight-night-102-albany-with-staph-infection-gian-villante|title=Patrick Cummins out of UFC Fight Night 102 card with staph infection|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-12-02|access-date=2016-12-02}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Saparbek Safarov]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/saparbek-safarov-j-j-aldrich-set-as-injury-replacements-at-ufc-fight-night-102-in-albany|title=Saparbek Safarov set as injury replacement at UFC Fight Night 102 in Albany|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-02|access-date=2016-12-02}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Justine Kish]] came in at 116.4 lb, 0.4 lb over the women's strawweight limit of 116 lb. As a result, Kish was fined 20% of her purse, which went to her opponent [[Ashley Yoder]] and the bout proceeded as scheduled at a catchweight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/ufc-fight-night-102-early-weigh-in-results-and-live-video-stream-9-a-m-et-1|title=UFC Fight Night 102 weigh-in results: Derrick Lewis (265.6), Shamil Abdurakhimov (253.8) official|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-08|access-date=2016-12-08}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (UFC Fight Pass)}}\n{{MMAevent 207: Nunes vs. Rousey\n| image = UFC_207_Poster.jpg\n| promotion = [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n| date = {{start date|2016|12|30}}\n| venue = [[T-Mobile Arena]]\n| city = [[Paradise, Nevada]]\n| attendance = 18,533{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/ufc-207-draws-sold-out-18533-attendance-a-las-vegas-ufc-record-for-4-75-million-live-gate|title=UFC 207 draws sold-out 18,533 attendance, a Las Vegas UFC record, for $4.75 million live gate|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-31|access-date=2016-12-31}}\n| gate= $4,750,000\n| buyrate = 1,100,000{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/1/8/14204530/ufc-207-does-over-1-million-buys|title=UFC 207 does over 1 million buys|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Rousey''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] (UFC) held on December 30, 2016, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas metropolitan area]].{{cite news|url=http://www.reviewjournal.com/sports/mma-ufc/ufc-207-set-las-vegas-december|title=UFC 207 set for Las Vegas in December|publisher=reviewjournal.com|author=Adam Hill|date=2016-08-19|access-date=2016-08-19}}\n\n==Background==\n[[File:Ronda Rousey retouch.jpg|thumb|left|140px|Rousey had her first fight since losing her title (and undefeated record) to [[Holly Holm]] at [[UFC 193]] in November 2015.]]\nThe event was headlined by a [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Amanda Nunes]] and former champion [[Ronda Rousey]].{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ronda-rousey-returns-at-ufc-207-to-face-amanda-nunes-101216|title=Ronda Rousey returns at UFC 207 to face Amanda Nunes|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-10-12|access-date=2016-10-12}}\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] bout between then champion [[Dominick Cruz]] and [[Cody Garbrandt]] co-headlined the event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/10/ufc-bantamweight-champ-dominick-cruz-meets-cody-garbrandt-in-ufc-207-co-headliner|title=UFC bantamweight champ Dominick Cruz meets Cody Garbrandt in UFC 207 co-headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2015-10-24|access-date=2015-10-24}}\n\nA potential heavyweight title eliminator bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship|UFC Heavyweight Champions]] [[Fabrício Werdum]] and [[Cain Velasquez]] was expected to take place on the card.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/9/15/12930664/fabricio-werdum-vs-cain-velasquez-2-targeted-for-ufc-207|title=Fabricio Werdum vs. Cain Velasquez 2 targeted for UFC 207|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-09-15|access-date=2016-09-15}} The pairing met previously in June 2015 at [[UFC 188]], with then interim champion Werdum unifying the title with Velasquez's undisputed title, as he won the fight via submission in the third round.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/06/ufc-188-results-fabricio-werdum-taps-out-cain-velasquez-for-shocking-title-upset|title=UFC 188 results: Fabricio Werdum taps out Cain Velasquez for shocking title upset|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2015-06-14|access-date=2016-10-21}} However the [[Nevada State Athletic Commission]], after analyzing physical examinations and interviews, deemed Velasquez unfit to compete due to prior injuries, and pulled him from the event on December 24. The bout was eventually scrapped, and Werdum was removed from the card as well.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/cain-velasquez-out-of-ufc-207-bout-with-fabrico-werdum|title=Cain Velasquez out of UFC 207 bout with Fabrico Werdum|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-24|access-date=2016-12-24}}\n\nAnother potential title eliminator included a bantamweight bout between former champion [[T.J. Dillashaw]] and [[John Lineker]].{{cite web |url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/17948948/tj-dillashaw-john-lineker-part-ufc-207|title=T.J. Dillashaw against John Lineker to be part of UFC 207|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2016-11-02|access-date=2016-11-02}}\n\n[[Matt Brown (fighter)|Matt Brown]] was expected to face former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Welterweight Championship|Strikeforce Welterweight Champion]] [[Tarec Saffiedine]] at the event.{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/10/18/13317784/matt-brown-vs-tarec-saffiedine-targeted-for-ufc-207|title=Matt Brown vs. Tarec Saffiedine targeted for UFC 207|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-10-18|access-date=2016-10-18}} However, Brown was pulled from the fight on November 11 in favor of a matchup with former [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] challenger [[Donald Cerrone]] three weeks earlier at [[UFC 206]].{{cite web |url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/11/11/13601562/donald-cerrone-vs-matt-brown-slated-for-ufc-206|title=Donald Cerrone vs. Matt Brown slated for UFC 206|author=Dave Doyle|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2016-11-11|access-date=2016-11-11}} Saffiedine instead faced [[Dong Hyun Kim]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/tarec-saffiedine-gets-replacement-dong-hyun-kim-at-ufc-207-in-las-vegas|title=Tarec Saffiedine gets replacement Dong Hyun Kim at UFC 207 in Las Vegas|author=Mike Bohn|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-11-16|access-date=2016-11-16}}\n\n[[Maryna Moroz]] was very briefly linked to a fight with [[Jéssica Andrade]] at the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2016/12/6/13854826/mma-news-jessica-andrade-vs-maryna-moroz-ufc-207|title=Andrade vs. Moroz not happening at UFC 207 (Updated)|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2016-12-06|access-date=2016-12-06|archive-date=2016-12-08|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20161208081157/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2016/12/6/13854826/mma-news-jessica-andrade-vs-maryna-moroz-ufc-207|url-status=dead}} On December 7 Moroz was replaced by [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] [[Angela Hill (fighter)|Angela Hill]].{{cite web |url=http://www.olimpomma.com/angela-hill-substitui-moroz-e-enfrenta-jessica-andrade-no-ufc-207/|title=Angela Hill replaces Moroz and faces Jéssica Andrade at UFC 207|author=Gabriel Carvalho|publisher=olimpomma.com|date=2016-12-07|access-date=2016-12-07|language=pt}} Hill was then ruled out of fighting at UFC 207 because of a rule in the UFC's anti-doping policy with USADA, but not because she had failed a drug test. The pair eventually fought at [[UFC Fight Night 104]], with Andrade winning by unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/12/13/13944112/angela-hill-s-ufc-return-on-hold-due-to-four-month-returning-fighter|title=Hill's UFC return on hold due to USADA rule|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-12-13|access-date=2016-12-13}} Subsequently, Andrade was removed from the card and was rescheduled for that event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/ufc-207-lineup-finalized-again-louis-smolka-vs-ray-borg-opens-main-card-on-pay-per-view|title=UFC 207 lineup finalized again: Louis Smolka vs. Ray Borg opens main card on pay-per-view|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-21|access-date=2016-12-21}}\n\n[[Sabah Homasi]] was linked to a fight with [[Brandon Thatch]] at the event.{{cite web |url=http://www.reviewjournal.com/sports/mma-ufc/welterweight-bout-added-ufc-207-card|title=Welterweight bout added to UFC 207 card|publisher=reviewjournal.com|author=Adam Hill|date=2016-10-17|access-date=2016-10-17}} However, Homasi pulled out due to injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Niko Price]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/sabah-homasi-out-brandon-thatch-meets-newcomer-niko-price-at-ufc-207/|title=Sabah Homasi out, Brandon Thatch meets newcomer Niko Price at UFC 207|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-12|access-date=2016-12-12}}\n\nThis was the last event in which longtime play-by-play commentator [[Mike Goldberg]] participated. He made his debut in 1997 at [[UFC Japan: Ultimate Japan]].{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/dana-white-confirms-ufc-207-is-mike-goldbergs-last-event-as-commentator|title=Dana White confirms UFC 207 is Mike Goldberg's last event as commentator|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco and John Morgan|date=2016-12-28|access-date=2016-12-28}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Johny Hendricks]] and [[Ray Borg]] missed weight for their bouts, weighing in at 173.5 lb and 129.5 lb, respectively. As a result, they were fined 20% and 30% of their respective fight purses, which went to their respective opponents [[Neil Magny]] and [[Louis Smolka]]. Later, Hendricks would move up in weight to the 185 pound division, as a result of consistent weight misses.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/ufc-207-early-weigh-in-results-and-live-video-stream-noon-et|title=UFC 207 early weigh-in results: Rousey hits 135, Hendricks and Borg miss weight|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-29|access-date=2016-12-29}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Bantamweight\n|[[Amanda Nunes]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[Ronda Rousey]]\n|TKO (punches)\n|1\n|0:48\n|For the [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[Cody Garbrandt]]\n|def.\n|[[Dominick Cruz]] (c)\n|Decision (unanimous) (48–46, 48–47, 48–46)\n|5\n|5:00\n|For the [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]].\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Bantamweight\n|[[T.J. Nunes vs. Rousey|publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship|access-date=2016-10-06}}\n\n==Bonus awards==\nThe following fighters were awarded $50,000 bonuses:{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/ufc-207-bonuses-amanda-nunes-and-alex-garcia-pick-up-50000-for-their-brutal-knockouts/|title=UFC 207 bonuses: Amanda Nunes and Alex Garcia pick up $50,000 for their brutal knockouts|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2016-12-31|date=2016-12-31|archive-date=2019-01-03|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20190103104802/https://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/ufc-207-bonuses-amanda-nunes-and-alex-garcia-pick-up-50000-for-their-brutal-knockouts|url-status=dead}}\n*'''Fight of the Night:''' '''Dominick Cruz vs. Cody Garbrandt'''\n*'''Performance of the Night:''' '''Amanda Nunes''' and '''Alex Garcia'''\n\n==Reported payout==\nThe following is the reported payout to the fighters as reported to the [[Nevada Athletic Commission|Nevada State Athletic Commission]]. It does not include sponsor money and also does not include the UFC's traditional \"fight night\" bonuses. The total disclosed payout for the event was $4,683,000.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/final-ufc-207-salaries-champ-amanda-nunes-gets-200k-ronda-rousey-3-million/|title=Final UFC 207 salaries: Champ Amanda Nunes gets $200K, Ronda Rousey $3 million|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-31|access-date=2016-12-31|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170101004829/http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/final-ufc-207-salaries-champ-amanda-nunes-gets-200k-ronda-rousey-3-million|archive-date=2017-01-01}} \n* '''Amanda Nunes:''' $200,000 (includes $100,000 win bonus) def. Ronda Rousey: $3,000,000\n* '''Cody Garbrandt:''' $200,000 (no win bonus) def. Dominick Cruz: $350,000\n* '''T.J. Dillashaw:''' $200,000 (includes $100,000 win bonus) def. John Lineker: $43,000\n* '''Dong Hyun Kim:''' $134,000 (includes $67,000 win bonus) def. Tarec Saffiedine: $40,000\n* '''Ray Borg:''' $30,600 (includes $18,000 win bonus) def. Louis Smolka: $37,400 ¹\n* '''Neil Magny:''' $114,000 (includes $47,000 win bonus) def. Johny Hendricks: $80,000 ²\n* '''Antônio Carlos Júnior:''' $42,000 (includes $21,000 win bonus) def. Marvin Vettori: $12,000\n* '''Alex Garcia:''' $36,000 (includes $18,000 win bonus) def. Mike Pyle: $55,000\n* '''Niko Price:''' $24,000 (includes $12,000 win bonus) def. Brandon Thatch: $22,000\n* Alex Oliveira: $28,000 vs. Tim Means: $35,000 ³\n\n¹ ''Ray Borg was fined 30 percent of his purse ($5,400) for failing to make the required weight for his fight with Louis Smolka. That money was issued to Smolka, an NSAC official confirmed.''
\n² ''Johny Hendricks was fined 20 percent of his purse ($20,000) for failing to make the required weight for his fight with Neil Magny. That money was issued to Magny, an NSAC official confirmed.''
\n³ ''Both fighters earned show money; no win bonus since fight ended in a no-contest.''\n\n==Records set==\nThe final attendance was 18,533, a record for [[Nevada]], which had hosted 107 prior UFC events. Rousey's fight purse equaled the highest in history, which was [[Conor McGregor]]'s record from [[UFC 202]]. Despite that, McGregor's [[UFC 205]] purse is widely known as the highest one (no value is officially disclosed), even though the [[NYSAC]] does not release fight purses from events held in their jurisdiction.\n\n==See also==\n\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2016 in UFC]]\n\n==References==\n{{Reflist|30em}}\n\n{{UFC Events}}\n\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship flagship built venue.\n\nA women's bout between [[Paige VanZant]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Atomweight Championship|Invicta FC Atomweight Champion]] [[Michelle Waterson]] served as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/10/paige-vanzant-headlines-ufc-on-fox-22-against-michelle-waterson|title=Paige VanZant headlines UFC on FOX 22 against Michelle viewers.[https://www.sportsbusinessdaily.com/Daily/Issues/2016/12/23/Media/Audience.aspx Audience Analysis: Fox Scores Best UFC Viewership Figure Since April '13] – Austin Karp, Sports Business Daily, Rousey]]\n|followingevent=[[UFC on Fox: Shevchenko vs. Peña]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Rodríguez vs. Penn''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 103''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on January 15, 2017, at [[Talking Stick Resort Arena]] in [[Phoenix, Arizona]]{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Visits-Phoenix-in-January|title=UFC visits Phoenix in January|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-11-04|access-date=2016-11-04}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/11/4/13526856/ufc-returns-to-phoenix-on-jan-15-three-fights-announced|title=UFC returns to Phoenix; three fights announced|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2016-11-04|access-date=2016-11-04}}\n\n==Background==\nThis was the second event the organization hosted in [[Phoenix, Arizona|Phoenix]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/john-moraga-vs-sergio-pettis-promoted-to-main-card-as-ufc-fight-night-103-finalized-with-12-bouts|title=John Moraga vs. Sergio Pettis promoted to main card as UFC Fight Night 103 finalized with 12 bouts|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-01-09|access-date=2017-01-09}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] featherweight winner [[Yair Rodríguez]] and former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight]] and [[UFC Welterweight Championship|Welterweight Champion]] [[B.J. Penn]] served as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Rodriguez-vs-Penn-to-Headline-Phoenix-Event|title=Rodríguez vs Penn to headline Phoenix event|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|access-date=2016-11-21|date=2016-11-21}} This was Penn's first fight in the organization in over two-and-a-half years.\n\n[[Erik Koch]] was expected to face [[Anthony Rocco Martin]] at the event. However, Koch pulled out of the fight on December 12 citing injury and was replaced by [[Alex White (fighter)|Alex White]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/alex-white-steps-in-for-erik-koch-against-tony-martin-at-ufc-fight-night-103|title=Alex White steps in for Erik Koch against Tony Martin at UFC Fight Night 103|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-12|access-date=2016-12-12}}\n\n[[Damian Grabowski]] was expected to face [[Viktor Pešta]] at the event. However, Grabowski was removed from the bout on December 21 for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Alexey Oleynik]].{{cite web|url=http://mmadna.nl/oleksiy-oliynyk-vervangt-damian-grabowski-tegen-viktor-pesta-in-phoenix/|title=Oleksiy Oliynyk replaces Damian Grabowski against Viktor Pesta in Phoenix|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2016-12-21|access-date=2016-12-21|language=nl}}\n\nOn December 29, two bouts suffered alterations: [[Jussier Formiga]] pulled out from his bout against [[Sergio Pettis]] and [[Jordan Rinaldi]] from his bout against [[Devin Powell]]. They were replaced by former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[John Moraga]] and promotional newcomer [[Drakkar Klose]], respectively.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/john-moraga-in-for-formiga-vs-sergio-pettis-drakkar-klose-meets-devin-powell-at-ufc-fight-night-103|title=John Moraga in for Formiga vs. Sergio Pettis, Drakkar Klose meets Devin Powell at UFC Fight Night 103|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-29|access-date=2016-12-29}}\n\n[[Bryan Caraway]] was expected to face [[Jimmie Rivera]] at the event. However, Caraway pulled out of the bout on January 4 citing an undisclosed injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/bryan-caraway-out-at-next-weeks-ufc-fight-night-103-jimmie-rivera-needs-new-opponent|title=Bryan Caraway out at next week's UFC Fight Night 103, Jimmie Rivera needs new opponent|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-01-04|access-date=2017-01-04}} He was replaced by [[Marlon Vera]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/marlon-vera-replaces-bryan-caraway-meets-jimmie-rivera-at-ufc-fight-night-103|title=Marlon Vera replaces Bryan Caraway, meets Jimmie Rivera at UFC Fight Night 103|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-01-06|access-date=2017-01-07}} Eventually, Rivera pulled from the fight as he felt going from a top-5 ranked fighter to an unranked one wouldn't make sense at that moment.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/1/7/14201248/jimmie-rivera-explains-why-he-wont-fight-replacement-marlon-vera-ill-look-like-a-bully|title=Jimmie Rivera explains why he wont fight replacement Marlon Vera: I'll look like a 'bully'|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2017-01-07|access-date=2017-01-08}}{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/jimmie-rivera-withdraws-from-ufc-fight-night-103-after-picking-marlon-vera-over-john-dodson|title=Jimmie Rivera withdraws from UFC Fight Night 103 after picking Marlon Vera over John Dodson|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-01-08|access-date=2017-01-08}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Featherweight\n|[[Yair Rodríguez]]\n|def.\n|[[B.J. Browne]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 208: Holm vs. de Randamie''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on February 11, 2017, at the [[Barclays Center]] in [[Brooklyn]], [[New York (state)|New York]].{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2016/12/1/13810318/ufc-reschedules-anaheim-event-brooklyn-card-is-now-ufc-208|title=UFC reschedules Anaheim event; Brooklyn card is now UFC 208|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=December 1, 2016|access-date=2016-12-01}}\n\n==Background==\n[[File:Barclays Center 1.jpg|thumb|left|250px|[[Barclays Center]] hosts games for teams like the [[Brooklyn Nets]] and [[New York Islanders]].]]\n\nThe event was originally scheduled to take place on January 21 at [[Honda Center]] in [[Anaheim]], California.{{cite news|url=https://www.dailynews.com/sports/20161109/ufc-208-announced-for-jan-21-at-honda-center|title=UFC 208 announced for Jan. 21 at Honda Center|publisher=dailynews.com|author=Brian Martin|date=November 9, 2016|access-date=2016-12-01}} However, due to a lack of suitable headliners for the date, the event in Anaheim was postponed to August 5 (and subsequently took place at [[Staples Center]] in Los Angeles) and an originally scheduled UFC 209 to be held at the [[Barclays Center]] in [[Brooklyn]], [[New York (state)|New York]] on February 11 was renamed as UFC 208.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/ufc-continues-to-map-out-early-2017-with-events-in-anaheim-denver-brooklyn-las-vegas|title=UFC continues to map out early 2017 with events in Anaheim, Denver, Brooklyn, Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=November 9, 2016|access-date=2016-11-15}} This was the first UFC event held in [[Brooklyn]] and the fourth overall hosted in the state of [[New York (state)|New York]].\n\nThe event was headlined by the inaugural [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship]] bout between former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Holly Holm]] and [[Germaine de Randamie]]. It was also the first women's featherweight bout in the promotion's history.{{cite news|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-208-introduces-women-featherweight-division-holm-derandamie-brooklyn|title=UFC introduces women's featherweight division with title fight in Brooklyn|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=December 13, 2016|access-date=2016-12-14}}{{cite news|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/18273574/holly-holm-germaine-de-randamie-fight-inaugural-ufc-featherweight-title|title=Holly Holm vs. Germaine de Randamie to feature 1st female UFC featherweight title|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=December 13, 2016|access-date=2016-12-13}}\n\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] unification bout between two-time champion [[José Aldo]] and interim champion [[Max Holloway]] was in the works for this card.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2016/12/12/13923428/jose-aldo-says-fighting-max-holloway-at-ufc-208|title=José Aldo says he's fighting Max Holloway at UFC 208|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=December 12, 2016|access-date=2016-12-12}} The bout eventually did not materialize as Holloway had some consideration (nagging injuries and the desire to spend Christmas with his family) about fighting only two months after [[UFC 206]].{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/manager-explains-why-max-holloway-isnt-fighting-jose-aldo-at-ufc-208|title=Manager explains why Max Holloway isn't fighting Jose Aldo at UFC 208|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=December 21, 2016|access-date=2016-12-28}}\n\nThe original Anaheim event had two announced bouts, including flyweights [[Wilson Reis]] and [[Ulka Sasaki]], as well as a light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Glover Teixeira]] and [[Jimi Manuwa]].{{cite web |title=Wilson Reis vs. Ulka Sasaki slated for UFC 208|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2016/11/25/13749188/wilson-reis-vs-ulka-sasaki-verbally-agreed-for-ufc-208|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=November 25, 2016|access-date=2016-11-25}}{{cite web |url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/18157341/glover-teixeira-jimi-manuwa-targeted-ufc-208-fight|title=Glover Teixeira, Jimi Manuwa agree to UFC 208 fight|author=Brett Okamoto|work=espn.com|date=November 28, 2016|access-date=2016-11-28}} Teixeira faced [[Jared Cannonier]] at the event, while Manuwa faced [[Corey Anderson (fighter)|Corey Anderson]] at [[UFC Fight Night 107]].{{cite web |title=Glover Teixeira vs. Jared Cannonier in the works for UFC 208|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2016/12/7/13874792/glover-teixeira-vs-jared-cannonier-in-the-works-for-ufc-208|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=December 7, 2016|access-date=2016-12-07}}\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Ian McCall (fighter)|Ian McCall]] and [[Neil Seery]] was originally scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Mousasi vs. Hall 2]]. However, the pairing was cancelled on the day of the weigh-ins, as McCall had to pull out of the fight after becoming ill due to the effects of his [[weight cutting|weight cut]].{{cite web |url=https://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/again-seriously-illness-forces-ian-mccall-out-of-ufc-fight-night-99-fight-with-neil-seery|title=Again? Seriously? Illness forces Ian McCall out of UFC Fight Night 99 fight with Neil Seery|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Matt Erickson|date=November 18, 2016|access-date=2016-11-18}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event. In turn, Seery pulled out on January 29 due to the passing of his mother-in-law.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/stop-us-if-youve-heard-this-one-ufc-208s-ian-mccall-loses-an-opponent-again/|title=Stop us if you've heard this one: UFC 208's Ian McCall loses an opponent – again|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=January 29, 2017|access-date=2017-01-29|archive-date=February 1, 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170201101946/http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/stop-us-if-youve-heard-this-one-ufc-208s-ian-mccall-loses-an-opponent-again/|url-status=dead}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Jarred Brooks]].{{cite web |url=https://www.ufc.com/news/Brooks-to-replace-Seery-at-UFC-208|title=Brooks to replace Seery at UFC 208|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=January 30, 2017|access-date=2017-01-30}} On the day of the event, McCall was hospitalized with gastrointestinal issues and the bout was cancelled.{{cite web |url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/cursed-ian-mccall-illness-scraps-ufc-208-fight-vs-jarred-brooks-no-seriously|title=Cursed: Ian McCall illness scraps UFC 208 fight vs. Jarred Brooks (no, seriously)|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=February 11, 2017|access-date=2017-02-11}}\n\n[[Gilbert Burns]] was expected to face [[Paul Felder]] at the event. However, Burns pulled out of the fight in mid-January citing injury.{{cite web |title=Gilbert Burns injured, UFC seeking replacement for Paul Felder|url=https://mymmanews.com/paul-felder-ufc-208/|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Eric Kowal|date=January 17, 2017|access-date=2017-01-17}} In turn, Felder was removed from the card and rescheduled against a new opponent at a separate event a week later.{{cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/1215761-mcmann-gets-carmouche-felder-faces-ricci-at-ufc-halifax|title=Paul Felder faces Alessandro Ricci at UFC Halifax|publisher=thescore.com|author=Themistoklis Alexis |date=January 23, 2016|access-date=2017-01-23}}\n\nA heavyweight contest between [[Derrick Lewis]] and [[Travis Browne]] was scheduled to take place at this event, but was moved a week later to headline [[UFC Fight Night 105]].{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/1/23/14364006/mma-news-travis-browne-vs-derrick-lewis-headlines-ufc-halifax|title=Browne vs Lewis rescheduled for UFC Fight Night 105|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=January 23, 2017|access-date=2017-01-23|archive-date=January 25, 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170125161743/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/1/23/14364006/mma-news-travis-browne-vs-derrick-lewis-headlines-ufc-halifax|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[George Sullivan (fighter)|George Sullivan]] was scheduled to face [[Randy Brown (fighter)|Randy Brown]] at the event. However, on January 26, Sullivan was pulled from the card after being notified by [[USADA]] of a potential anti-doping violation stemming from an out-of-competition sample collected earlier this year.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/george-sullivan-notified-of-potential-usada-anti-doping-violation-out-of-ufc-208-fight-with-randy-brown|title=George Sullivan notified of potential USADA anti-doping violation, out of UFC 208 fight with Randy Brown|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=January 26, 2017|access-date=2017-01-26}} [[Belal Muhammad]] was chosen as his replacement.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/with-sullivan-out-randy-brown-now-meets-belal-muhammad-at-ufc-208-in-brooklyn|title=With Sullivan out, Randy Brown now meets Belal Muhammad at UFC 208 in Brooklyn|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=February 4, 2017|access-date=2017-02-04}}\n\n[[Luis Henrique (fighter)|Luis Henrique]] was expected to face [[Marcin Tybura]], but was pulled from the bout on February 1 due to NYSAC concerns related to a corrective eye surgery he underwent almost a year before.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/luis-henrique-out-at-next-weeks-ufc-208-promotion-needs-heavyweight-opponent-for-marcin-tybura|title=Luis Henrique out at next week's UFC 208; promotion needs heavyweight opponent for Marcin Tybura|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=February 1, 2017|access-date=2017-02-01}}{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/scratched-from-ufc-208-due-to-eye-issue-luis-henrique-says-his-career-options-now-limited|title=Scratched from UFC 208 due to eye issue, Luis Henrique says his career options now limited|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco and Christian Stein|date=February 6, 2017|access-date=2017-02-06}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Justin Willis (martial artist)|Justin Willis]].{{cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/Willis-replaces-Henrique-faces-Tybura-at-UFC-208|title=Willis replaces Henrique, faces Tybura at UFC 208|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=February 7, 2017|access-date=2017-02-07}} In turn, the bout was cancelled entirely as Willis was deemed \"medically unfit\" to compete.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/justin-wilis-out-of-UFC-208-fight-with-marcin-tybura|title=Willis out of UFC fight with Tybura|publisher=ufc.com|author=Matt Parrino|date=February 10, 2017|access-date=2017-02-10}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Featherweight\n|[[Germaine de Randamie]]\n|def.\n|[[Holly Holm]]\n|Decision web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/ufc-208-bonuses-with-the-only-finish-on-the-card-naturally-jacare-souza-got-an-extra-50000/|title=UFC 208 bonuses: With the only finish on the card, naturally 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170212085209/http://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/ufc-208-bonuses-with-the-only-finish-on-the-card-naturally-jacare-souza-got-an-extra-50000|url-status=dead}}\n*'''Fight of the Night: Dustin Poirier vs. Jim Miller'''\n*'''Performance of the Night:''' '''Ronaldo Souza'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn February 15, it was announced that Holm filed a complaint against referee Todd Anderson and an appeal of the result, asking the NYSAC to review Anderson's \"failure to deduct one or more points from Germaine de Randamie following her repeated strikes thrown after the horn sounded to end rounds 2 and 3.\"{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/2/15/14628304/holly-holm-officially-appeals-ufc-208-loss-due-to-punches-landed-after-horn|title=Holly Holm officially appeals UFC 208 loss due to punches landed after horn|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=February 15, 2017|access-date=2017-02-15}} Thirteen days later, the NYSAC denied Holm's appeal.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/2/28/14766046/nysac-denies-holly-holms-appeal-of-ufc-208-loss|title=NYSAC denies Holly Holm's appeal of UFC 208 loss|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=February 28, 2017|access-date=2017-02-28}}\n\nOn June 20, Sullivan was officially suspended for his second anti-doping offense by USADA, receiving a one-year term for his failed test. He had disclosed he was taking prescription fertility medication and provided medical records to document his treatment, which wound up helping him when punishment was considered. His positive test was caused by [[clomiphene citrate]], which \"he was using in a therapeutic dose under the care of a physician to treat a medical condition,\" USADA said in a statement. They determined that Sullivan's degree of fault was reduced because \"his use of clomiphene citrate was under the care of his physician for a documented medical condition. Accordingly, Sullivan received a reduction to one year from the standard two-year period of ineligibility that could have been imposed for a second offense involving a specified substance.\" His period of ineligibility began January 14, the date of his positive test.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/ufc-welterweight-george-sullivan-1-year-ban-usadas|title=USADA hands UFC's George Sullivan 1-year suspension for 2nd anti-doping offense|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=June 20, 2017|access-date=2017-06-21}}{{Dead link|date=January 2026 |bot=InternetArchiveBot estimatedviewers =\n| purse =\n| previousevent = [[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Browne]]\n| followingevent = [[UFC Fight Night: Belfort vs. Gastelum]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 209: Woodley vs. Thompson 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on March 4, 2017, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas metropolitan area]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/12/1/13810318/ufc-reschedules-anaheim-event-brooklyn-card-is-now-ufc-208|title=UFC reschedules Anaheim event; Brooklyn card is now UFC 208|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2016-12-01|access-date=2016-12-01}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was originally scheduled to take place on February 11 at [[Barclays Center]] in [[Brooklyn]], [[New York (state)|New York]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/ufc-continues-to-map-out-early-2017-with-events-in-anaheim-denver-brooklyn-las-vegas|title=UFC continues to map out early 2017 with events in Anaheim, Denver, Brooklyn, Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-11-09|access-date=2016-11-15}} However, due to a lack of suitable headliners for the original [[UFC 208]] (scheduled for [[Anaheim]], [[California]]), that event was postponed to July 29 and an originally scheduled UFC 209 to be held in Brooklyn was renamed as the new UFC 208. Therefore, this event was also renamed from UFC 210 to UFC 209. This was the fourth UFC event held in the venue.\n\nA number of fans, including longtime UFC commentator [[Joe Rogan]], had expressed hope that UFC 209 would feature [[Nick Diaz|Nick]] and/or [[Nate Diaz]] on the card or be held in or near their hometown of [[Stockton, California]]. 209 is [[Area code 209|the telephone area code for Stockton]]: the brothers have long expressed pride in being from \"the 209\", and Nate Diaz had even once yelled \"209! 209!\" at [[Clay Guida]] during their fight as trash talk.{{Cite web|url=https://talksport.com/sport/mma/117465/nate-diaz-209-stockton-ufc-fight/|title=Nate Diaz once confused opponent during a fight by repeatedly yelling '209'|date=November 2019}} Rogan even went so far as to say that a Diaz-based UFC 209 could break the company's pay-per-view buy record. Ultimately, the UFC could not come to financial terms with either Diaz brother, and the [[Stockton Arena]] was deemed too small for a major pay-per-view event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.forbes.com/sites/mattconnolly/2017/02/24/joe-rogan-ufc-209-with-nick-and-nate-diaz-would-have-broken-pay-per-view-record/#354940cb72a8|title = Joe Rogan: Nick Diaz and Nate Diaz on UFC 209 Would Break UFC PPV Record|website = [[Forbes]]}}\n\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] rematch between current champion [[Tyron Woodley]] and five-time [[kickboxing]] world champion [[Stephen Thompson (fighter)|Stephen Thompson]] headlined this event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/welterweight-champ-tyron-woodley-rematch-with-stephen-thompson-set-for-ufc-209|title=Welterweight champ Tyron Woodley rematch with Stephen Thompson headlines UFC 209|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-01-09|access-date=2017-01-10}} The pairing met recently at [[UFC 205]], as Woodley retained his title after the fight ended in a majority draw.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/11/13/13612694/ufc-205-results-tyron-woodley-retains-title-in-wild-draw-with-stephen|title=UFC 205 results: Tyron Woodley retains title in wild draw with Stephen Thompson|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2016-11-13|access-date=2017-01-10}}\n\nAn interim [[UFC Lightweight Championship]] bout between undefeated [[Khabib Nurmagomedov]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Lesnar vs. Team dos Santos]] welterweight winner [[Tony Ferguson]] was expected to take place at the co-main event.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/1/12/14259356/khabib-nurmagomedov-vs-tony-ferguson-official-for-ufc-209|title=Khabib Nurmagomedov vs. Tony Ferguson official for UFC 209|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2017-01-12|access-date=2017-01-12}} The pairing was originally booked for [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber#The Ultimate Fighter 22 Finale|The Ultimate Fighter: Team McGregor vs. Team Faber Finale]] and later [[UFC on Fox: Teixeira vs. Evans]]. However, the bout was cancelled both times due to Nurmagomedov being injured{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2015/10/30/9645620/edson-barboza-steps-in-for-khabib-nurmagomedov-to-face-tony-ferguson|title=Edson Barboza steps in for Khabib Nurmagomedov to face Tony Ferguson at TUF Finale|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2015-10-30|access-date=2017-01-14}} and Ferguson pulling out due to a [[lung]] issue.{{cite web|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/ufc-tony-ferguson-out-rashad-evans-vs-glover-teixeira-set-as-new-main-event-040516|title=Tony Ferguson out; Rashad Evans vs. Glover Teixeira set as new main event|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2016-04-05|access-date=2017-01-14}} Once again the bout suffered a cancellation, as Nurmagomedov had medical issues related to his weight cut and the doctors opted to pull him from the event on weigh-in day.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/3/3/14805818/ufc-releases-statement-on-khabib-nurmagomedov|title=UFC releases statement on Khabib Nurmagomedov|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2017-03-03|access-date=2017-03-03}}\n\n[[Igor Pokrajac]] was expected to face [[Ed Herman (fighter)|Ed Herman]] at the event. However, Pokrajac pulled out of the fight in early February citing an injury, and was replaced by [[Gadzhimurad Antigulov]].{{cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/igor-pokrajac-out-gadzimurad-antigulov-in-tegen-ed-herman-tijdens-ufc-209/|title=Igor Pokrajac out, Gadzimurad Antigulov against Ed Herman at UFC 209|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2017-02-07|access-date=2017-02-16|language=nl}} Then, on February 20, Herman revealed that he was injured and also unable to compete at the event.{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Ed-Herman-Reveals-Injury-[[Gadzhimurad-Antigulov]]-in-Need-for-New-Opponent-for-UFC-209-on-March-9-117677|title=Ed Herman reveals injury, Gadzhimurad Antigulov in need for new opponent for UFC 209 on March 9|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jordan Breen|date=2017-02-20|access-date=2017-02-20}} In turn, promotion officials elected to remove Antigulov from the card and he will be rescheduled for a future event.{{cite web|url=http://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-209-Lineup-Finalized-with-Addition-of-Two-New-Bouts-117783|title=UFC 209 lineup finalized with addition of two new bouts|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2017-02-23|access-date=2017-02-23}}\n\n[[Todd Duffee]] was expected to face [[Mark Godbeer]] at the event. However, Duffee pulled out of the fight in mid-February for undisclosed reasons.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/mark-godbeer-says-opponent-todd-duffee-out-of-ufc-209-matchup|title=Mark Godbeer says opponent Todd Duffee out of UFC 209 matchup|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-02-16|access-date=2017-02-16}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Daniel Spitz]].{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/2/17/14651974/daniel-spitz-replaces-todd-duffee-at-ufc-209|title=Daniel Spitz replaces Todd Duffee at UFC 209|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Dave Doyle|date=2017-02-17|access-date=2017-02-19}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Marcin Tybura]] and [[Luis Henrique (fighter)|Luis Henrique]], initially scheduled for [[UFC 208]], was moved to this event. The pairing was initially delayed as Henrique was unable to gain medical clearance by the [[NYSAC]] after a recent corrective eye surgery.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmarocks.pl/marcin-tybura/marcin-tybura-%E2%80%8Fufc-209|title=Marcin Tybura vs. Luis Henrique at UFC 209|publisher=mmarocks.pl|author=Tomasz between multiple-time [[muay thai]] world champion [[Valentina Shevchenko]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate]] bantamweight winner [[Julianna Peña]] served as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/valentina-shevchenko-vs-julianna-pena-headlines-fox-ufc-card-in-denver-120616|title=Valentina Shevchenko vs. Julianna Pena headlines FOX originally booked for [[UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Abdurakhimov]]. However, Sterling pulled out due to an undisclosed injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/aljamain-sterling-out-at-ufc-fight-night-102-promotion-needs-u-s-based-foe-for-raphael-assuncao-2|title=Aljamain Sterling out at UFC Fight Night 102, promotion needs U.S.-based foe for Raphael Assuncao|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=November 24, 2016|access-date=2016-12-08}} The fight was later rescheduled for this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/12/7/13876136/raphael-assuncao-vs-aljamain-sterling-slated-for-ufc-on-fox-23|title=Raphael Assuncao vs. Aljamian Sterling slated for UFC on Fox 23|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=December 7, 2016|access-date=2016-12-08}}\n\n[[Yancy Medeiros]] was expected to face [[Li Jingliang]] at the event. As a result, Lima was fined 20% of his purse, which went to Kimball and the bout proceeded as scheduled at catchweight.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/ufc-fight-night-and-super-bowl-li-will-share-a-weekend-in-houston|title=UFC Fight Night and Super Bowl LI will share a weekend in Championship]] challenger [[The Korean Zombie|Chan Sung Jung]] served as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/12/2/13817968/chan-sung-jung-vs-dennis-bermudez-targeted-for-super-bowl-weekend|title=Chan Sung Jung vs. Dennis Bermudez targeted for Super Bowl weekend headliner|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2016-12-02|access-date=2016-12-02}} This was Jung's first bout since August 2013, as he returned from [[Conscription in South Korea|military service]].\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Evan Dunham]] and [[Abel Trujillo]] was originally booked for [[UFC Fight Night: Poirier vs. Johnson]]. However, Trujillo pulled out due to an undisclosed injury.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/09/ex-wsof-champ-rick-glenn-signs-with-ufc-meets-evan-dunham-at-ufc-fight-night-94|title=With
