this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/The-Korean-Zombie-headlines-UFC-return-to-Houston|title=The Korean Zombie headlines UFC return to Houston|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|date=2016-12-03|access-date=2016-12-04}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Jéssica Andrade]] and [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] [[Angela Hill]] was briefly linked to [[UFC 207]].{{cite web |url=http://www.olimpomma.com/angela-hill-substitui-moroz-e-enfrenta-jessica-andrade-no-ufc-207/|title=Angela Hill replaces Moroz and faces Jéssica Andrade at UFC 207|author=Gabriel Carvalho|publisher=olimpomma.com|date=2016-12-07|access-date=2016-12-07|language=pt}} However the fight never materialized for that event because of a rule in the UFC's anti-doping policy with USADA.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/12/13/13944112/angela-hill-s-ufc-return-on-hold-due-to-four-month-returning-fighter|title=Hill's UFC return on hold due to USADA rule|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2016-12-13|access-date=2016-12-13}} Subsequently, Andrade was removed from that card with the pairing left intact and rescheduled to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmarising.com/news/2017/01/15/jessica-andrade-vs-angela-hill-planned-for-ufc-fight-night-104/|title=Jéssica Andrade vs Angela Hill planned for UFC Fight Night 104|publisher=mmarising.com|author=Robert Sargent|date=2017-01-16|access-date=2017-01-17}}\n\nOn January 19, the card suffered multiple changes due to injury: [[Evan Dunham]] and [[Johnny Case]] pulled out of their respective fights against [[Abel Trujillo]] and [[James Vick]]. Subsequently, Trujillo and Vick were booked to face each other.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/1/19/14324378/after-injuries-to-original-opponents-james-vick-and-abel-trujillo-to-fight-in-houston|title=After injuries to original opponents, James Vick and Abel Trujillo fight in Houston|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=2017-01-19|access-date=2017-01-19}} Also, [[Sheldon Westcott]] was forced to withdraw from his bout against [[Alex Morono]] at the event.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/sheldon-westcott-out-at-ufc-fight-night-104-alex-morono-needs-welterweight-opponent-in-houston|title=Sheldon Westcott out at UFC Fight Night 104, Alex Morono needs welterweight opponent in Houston|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=2017-01-19|access-date=2017-01-19}} He was replaced by [[Niko Price]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/niko-price-replaces-sheldon-westcott-at-ufc-fight-night-104-in-houston|title=Niko Price replaces Sheldon Westcott at UFC Fight Night 104 in Houston|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-01-24|access-date=2017-01-24}}\n\n[[Jan Błachowicz]] was expected to face former interim [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Ovince Saint Preux]], but pulled out on January 21 due to injury and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Volkan Oezdemir]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/ovince-saint-preux-gets-new-opponent-ufcs-first-switzerland-native-for-ufc-fight-night-104|title=Ovince Saint Preux gets new opponent – UFC's first Switzerland native – for UFC Fight Night 104|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-01-21|access-date=2017-01-21}}\n\n[[Justin Ledet]] was expected to face promotional newcomer Dmitry Sosnovskiy at the event. However, Ledet pulled out of the fight on January 26 allegedly due to an undisclosed injury. Promotion officials elected to remove Sosnovskiy from the card and he will be rescheduled for another event.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmanews.com/report-justin-ledet-off-ufc-fight-night-card-in-houston-with-an-injury/|title=Justin Ledet off UFC Fight Night card in Houston with an injury|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Fernando Quiles Jr.|date=2017-01-26|access-date=2017-01-26}}{{cite web|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/ufc-statement-on-justin-ledet-020117|title=UFC statement on Justin Ledet |publisher=ufc.com|author=UFC press release|date=2017-02-01|access-date=2017-02-01}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Bec Rawlings]] came in at 117.5 lb, 1.5 lb over the women's strawweight limit of 116 lb. As a result, Rawlings was fined 20% of her purse, which went to [[Tecia Torres]] and the bout proceeded as scheduled at catchweight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/ufc-fight-night-104-weigh-in-results-and-live-video-stream-10-a-m-et-1-1|title=UFC Fight Night 104 weigh-in results: Bermudez (145.5) and Jung (145.5) official, Rawlings off mark|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-02-03|access-date=2017-02-03}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent bout|Featherweight \n|[[The Korean Zombie|Chan Sung Jung]]\n|def.\n|[[Dennis Bermudez]]\n|KO (punch)\n|1\n|2:49\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight \n|[[Felice Herrig]]\n|def.\n|[[Alexa Grasso]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (29–28, 29–28, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Lightweight \n|[[James Vick]]\n|def.\n|[[Abel Trujillo]]\n|Submission (D'Arce choke)\n|3\n|0:49\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Volkan Oezdemir]]\n|def.\n|[[Ovince Saint Preux]]\n|Decision (split) (29–28, 28–29, 29–28)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Marcel Fortuna]]\n|def.\n|[[Anthony Hamilton (fighter)|Anthony Hamilton]]\n|KO (punch)\n|1\n|3:10\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Women's Strawweight\n|[[Jéssica Andrade]]\n|def.\n|[[Angela Hill]]\n|Decision (unanimous) (30–27, 30–27, 30–27)\n|3\n|5:00\n|\n}}\n{{MMAevent card|Preliminary Card (Fox Sports 1)|header=no}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Curtis Blaydes]]\n|vs.\n|[[Adam Milstead]]\n|No Contest (overturned)\n|2\n|0:59\n|Originally a TKO (knee injury) win for Blaydes; All three have been suspended 90 days and fined $1,000 by the Texas Department of Licensing and Regulation (TDLR), sources said. Despite their test failures for marijuana, none are facing any sanctions from USADA, as it only suspends fighters for cannabis if their drug test comes back with more than 150 ng/ml of the substance’s metabolites. Victories by Price and Blaydes have been overturned to no contests.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/3/17/14962180/three-fighters-test-positive-for-marijuana-at-ufc-fight-night-104|title=Three fighters test positive for marijuana at UFC Fight Night 104|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2017-03-17|access-date=2017-03-17}}\n\nOn April 5, it was announced that Ledet accepted a four-month USADA's sanction for an anti-doping policy violation following an out-of-competition test conducted on January 12. He tested positive for 5α-androst-1-ene-3α-ol-17-one, a metabolite of [[1-testosterone]] and [[1-androstenedione]]. He provided USADA with an open container of a dietary supplement product he was using at the time of the relevant sample collection, which he had also declared on his sample collection paperwork and researched before using. The testing conclusively confirmed that the supplement Ledet was using at the time of his positive test was contaminated with 1-androstenedione. The period of ineligibility began on February 1, 2017, the date on which he was provisionally suspended from competition.{{cite web|url=http://ufc.usada.org/justin-ledet-accepts-doping-sanction/|title=UFC athlete, Justin Ledet, accepts sanction for anti-doping policy violation|publisher=ufc.usada.org|author=Staff|date=2017-04-05|access-date=2017-04-06|archive-date=2017-04-06|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170406004457/http://ufc.usada.org/justin-ledet-accepts-doping-sanction/|url-status=dead}}\n\n==See [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on March 11, 2017, at the Centro de Formação Olímpica do Nordeste in [[Fortaleza]], [[Brazil]].{{cite hosted in [[Ceará]], and first since [[UFC on Fuel TV: Nogueira vs. Werdum]] which was held at [[Ginásio Paulo Sarasate]] in June 2013. This event was expected to be UFC Fight Night 107, but an initially planned UFN 106 in [[Las Vegas|Las Vegas, Nevada]] never materialized, prompting the change in numbers.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/ufc-ditches-plans-for-march-3-event-in-las-vegas-on-eve-of-ufc-209|title=UFC ditches plans for March 3 event in Las Vegas on eve of UFC 209|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|access-date=2017-01-09|date=2017-01-10}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship|UFC Light Heavyweight Champion]] and [[List of UFC Champions#Tournament Winners|UFC 12 Heavyweight Tournament winner]] [[Vitor Belfort]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] middleweight winner [[Kelvin Gastelum]] was the event headliner.{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Gastelum-faces-Belfort-in-Fortaleza-main-event|title=Gastelum faces Belfort in Fortaleza main event|publisher=ufc.com|author=Thomas Gerbasi|access-date=2016-12-14|date=2016-12-14}}\n\n[[Ion Cuțelaba]] was briefly linked to a bout against [[Henrique da Silva (fighter)|Henrique da Silva]] at the event. However the bout never materialized due to an undisclosed injury for Cuțelaba. da Silva was expected to remain on the card against another opponent, but elected to take another fight at a separate event.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/luis-henrique-da-silva-expected-to-fight-at-ufc-fight-night-107-just-not-against-ion-cutelaba|title=Luis Henrique da Silva expected to fight at UFC Fight Night 107 – just not against Ion Cutelaba|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2017-01-06|date=2017-01-06}}{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/1/10/14229234/luis-henrique-da-silva-jordan-johnson-ufc-on-fox-23|title=Luis Henrique da Silva faces RFA champion Jordan Johnson at UFC on FOX 23|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2017-01-10|access-date=2017-01-10}}\n\n[[Alex Nicholson (fighter)|Alex Nicholson]] was briefly linked to a bout with promotional newcomer [[Paulo Costa]] at the event. However, Nicholson was removed from the pairing in favor of [[Garreth McLellan]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/garreth-mclellan-quickly-replaces-alex-nicholson-at-ufc-fight-night-106|title=Garreth McLellan quickly replaces Alex Nicholson at UFC Fight Night 106|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-01-20|access-date=2017-01-20}}\n\n[[Max Griffin]] was expected to face [[Sérgio Moraes]] at the event. However, both fighters pulled out of the fight during the week leading up to the event citing injuries and the bout was scrapped.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/godofredo-pepey-vs-kyle-bochniak-scrapped-from-ufc-fight-night-106-in-brazil|title=Godofredo Pepey vs. Kyle Bochniak scrapped from UFC Fight Night 106 in '''Edson Barboza''', '''Michel Prazeres''' and '''Paulo Costa'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nGastelum was expected to face former [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Anderson Silva]] at [[UFC 212]] on June 3. However, on April 6, he was pulled from the bout after he was flagged for a potential doping violation by [[USADA]] stemming from his bout against Belfort at this event. He tested positive for Carboxy-Tetrahydrocannabinol (Carboxy-THC) which is a metabolite of [[marijuana]] and/or [[hashish]] above the 180 ng/mL allowance by the [[World Anti-Doping Agency]] (WADA) standard. The in-competition sample was collected on the night of this event. Gastelum was placed on a provisional suspension as a result of the positive drug test and he was expected to be afforded a full adjudication process.{{cite news|url=http://www.foxsports.com/ufc/story/kelvin-gastelum-pulled-from-fight-with-anderson-silva-after-testing-positive-for-marijuana-040617|title=Kelvin Gastelum pulled from fight with Anderson Silva after testing positive for marijuana|work=foxsports.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2017-04-06|access-date=2017-04-06}} On May 7, Gastelum's win was officially overturned and he was suspended 90 days (retroactive to the March 11 test date), and he's been fined 20 percent of his purse (which wasn't disclosed). The fine will be held by the commission.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/kelvin-gastelum-win-overturned-vitor-belfort|title=Kelvin Gastelum's win over Vitor Belfort overturned, 90-day suspension issued|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Fernanda Prates and Dann Stupp|date=2017-05-07|access-date=2017-05-07}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n*[[2017 in prompting the change in numbers.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/ufc-ditches-plans-for-march-3-event-in-las-vegas-on-eve-of-ufc-209|title=UFC ditches plans for March 3 event in Las Vegas London|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=29 January 2017|access-date=30 January 2017}}\n\n[[Brad Pickett]] was expected to face [[Henry Briones]], in what would be his retirement bout.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmamania.com/2016/12/26/14082934/ufc-brad-pickett-wants-retire-ufc-fight-night-108-london-mma|title=Brad Pickett wants to retire at UFC Fight Night 108 in London|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Adam Guillen Jr|date=26 December 2016|access-date=20 February 2017}} However, Briones pulled out a week before the event and was replaced by [[Marlon Vera]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/brad-pickett-gets-new-opponent-for-next-weeks-retirement-fight-at-ufc-fight-night-107|title=Brad Pickett gets new foe for next week's retirement fight at UFC 140 lb.{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/Pickett-Vera-to-proceed-as-catchweight-bout|title=Pickett-Vera to proceed as catchweight bout|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=17 March 2017|access-date=17 March 2017}} After this fight, Pickett placed his gloves on the canvas of the octagon and announced his retirement.\n\n[[Veronica Macedo]] was expected to face [[Lina Länsberg]] at the event. However, Macedo pulled out of the fight on February 28.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/veronica-macedo-out-promotion-seeks-new-opponent-for-lina-lansberg-at-ufc-fight-night-107|title=Veronica Macedo out, promotion seeks new opponent for Lina Lansberg at UFC Fight Night 107|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=28 February 2017|access-date=28 February 2017}} She was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Lucie Pudilová]]. The fighters first met in 2015, when Länsberg won via unanimous decision at Battle of Botnia 2015 in Sweden.{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/lina-lansberg-gets-new-but-familiar-opponent-for-next-weeks-ufc-fight-night-107|title=Lina Lansberg gets new (but familiar) opponent for next week's UFC Fight Night 107|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=11 March 2017|access-date=11 March 2017}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Ian Entwistle]] came in at 139 lb, three pounds over the bantamweight limit of 136 lb. As a result, he was fined 20% of his purse, which would go to his opponent [[Brett Johns]] and the bout would proceed at a catchweight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/ufc-fight-night-107-official-weigh-in-video-photos-one-punch-accepts-catchweight-in-final-fight-entwistle-comes-up-short|title=UFC Fight Night 107 official weigh-in results: 'One Punch' accepts catchweight in final fight, Entwistle comes up short|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Abby Subhan|date=17 March 2017|access-date=17 March 2017}} However, Entwistle was pulled from the fight on the day of the event and the bout was cancelled.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/ian-entwistle-ruled-out-of-ufc-fight-night-107-bout-with-brett-johns-scrapped|title=Ian Entwistle ruled out of UFC Fight Night 107, bout with Brett Johns scrapped|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=18 March 2017|access-date=18 March 2017|archive-date=28 December 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20171228172309/http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/ian-entwistle-ruled-out-of-ufc-fight-night-107-bout-with-brett-johns-scrapped|url-status=dead}}\n\nMoments before the start of the event, [[Tom Breese]] was deemed unfit to compete and was pulled from his bout against [[Oluwale Bamgbose]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/03/oluwale-bamgbose-vs-tom-breese-also-pulled-from-todays-ufc-fight-night-107-lineup/|title=Oluwale Bamgbose vs. Tom Breese also pulled from today's UFC Fight Night Championship challenger[[Jorge Masvidal]] was initially targeted to headline this event.{{cite Morgan|date=2017-02-28|access-date=2017-04-17}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Marcos Rogério de Lima]] came in at 210 lb, four pounds over the light heavyweight limit of 206 lb. description|UFC mixed martial arts event in 2017}}\n{{Infobox MMA event\n|name=UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Browne\n|image=UFC_Halifax.jpg\n|promotion=[[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2017|02|19}}\n|venue=[[Scotiabank Centre]]\n|city=[[Halifax, Nova Scotia]], [[Canada]]\n|attendance=8,123{{cite Thompson 2]]\n}}\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Browne''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 105''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event promoted by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on February 19, 2017, at [[Scotiabank Centre]] in [[Halifax, Nova Scotia]], [[Canada]].{{cite news|url=http://www.sportsnet.ca/mma/ufc-returning-halifax-february-televised-fight-night-event|title=UFC returning to Halifax in February with televised Fight Night event|publisher=sportsnet.ca|author=Canadian Press|date=2016-11-08|access-date=2016-11-08}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the second that the promotion hosted in [[Nova Scotia]], and the first since [[UFC Fight Night: MacDonald vs. Saffiedine]] in October 2014.\n\nA heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Champion]] [[Junior dos Santos]] and longtime divisional contender [[Stefan Struve]] was originally expected to headline the event. The pairing met once before in February 2009 at [[UFC 95]], where Dos Santos defeated Struve (who was making his UFC debut) via TKO in the first round.{{cite news|url=http://www.ufc.com/news/UFC-Returns-to-Halifax-with-Dynamic-Heavyweight-Match-Up|title=UFC returns to Halifax with dynamic heavyweight matchup|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-14|access-date=2016-12-14}}{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/12/junior-dos-santos-vs-stefan-struve-rematch-headlines-ufc-fight-night-105-in-halifax|title=Junior Dos Santos vs. Stefan Struve rematch headlines UFC Fight Night 105 in Halifax|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2016-12-14|access-date=2016-12-14}} However, on January 12, Struve pulled out due to a shoulder injury.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/1/12/14258154/stefan-struve-injured-out-of-ufc-halifax-bout-against-junior-dos-santos|title=Stefan Struve injured, out of UFC Halifax bout against JDS|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2017-01-12|access-date=2017-01-12}} Despite having over six weeks before the event, promotion officials elected to remove Dos Santos.{{cite news|url=http://sportv.globo.com/site/combate/noticia/2017/01/ufc-nao-consegue-substituto-para-struve-e-cigano-deixa-card-do-canada.html|title=UFC can't find a substitute for Struve, and Cigano leaves Canada's card|publisher=sportv.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|access-date=2017-01-18|date=2017-01-18|language=pt}} \n\nNearly two weeks later, it was announced that the new headliner would be a heavyweight matchup between [[Derrick Lewis]] and [[Travis Browne]]. The bout was scheduled to take place at [[UFC 208]], but was moved a week later to headline this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/1/23/14364006/mma-news-travis-browne-vs-derrick-lewis-headlines-ufc-halifax|title=Browne vs Lewis rescheduled for UFC Fight Night 105|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Mookie Alexander|date=2017-01-23|access-date=2017-01-23|archive-date=2017-01-25|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170125161743/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/1/23/14364006/mma-news-travis-browne-vs-derrick-lewis-headlines-ufc-halifax|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Álvaro Herrera]] was expected to face Alessandro Ricci at the event. However, Herrera pulled out of the fight in mid-January citing injury and was replaced by [[Paul Felder]].{{cite web|url=https://www.thescore.com/mma/news/1215761-mcmann-gets-carmouche-felder-faces-ricci-at-ufc-halifax|title=Paul Felder faces Alessandro Ricci at UFC Halifax|publisher=thescore.com|author=Themistoklis Alexis|date=2016-01-23|access-date=2017-01-23}}\n\nFormer [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Liz Carmouche]] was expected to face [[Wrestling at the 2004 Summer Olympics|2004 Olympic silver medalist]] in wrestling and fellow title challenger [[Sara McMann]] at the event, but pulled out on February 2. She was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Gina Mazany]].{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/carmouche-out-gina-mazany-meets-mcmann-at-ufc-fight-night-105-quinones-volkanovski-added|title=Carmouche out, Gina Mazany meets McMann at UFC Fight Night 105; Quinones-Volkanovski added|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-02-03|access-date=2017-02-03}}\n\nMichel Quiñones was expected to face [[Alexander Volkanovski]] at the event, but Volkanovski announced on February 13 that the bout was scrapped due to Quiñones being injured and no replacement being found.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/alex-volkanovski-says-hes-off-sundays-ufc-fight-night-105-card-in-halifax-due-to-opponent-issues/|title=Alex Volkanovski says he's off Sunday's UFC Fight Night 105 card in Halifax due to opponent issues|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-02-13|access-date=2017-02-13|archive-date=2017-02-14|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170214010554/http://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/alex-volkanovski-says-hes-off-sundays-ufc-fight-night-105-card-in-halifax-due-to-opponent-issues|url-status=dead}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, Mazany came in at 139.5 lb, 3.5 lb over the women's bantamweight limit of 136 lb. As a result, she was fined 20% of her purse, which went to McMann and the bout proceeded as scheduled at catchweight.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/ufc-fight-night-105-early-weigh-in-results-and-live-video-stream-8-a-m-et|title=UFC Fight Night 105 official weigh-in results: Browne (241), Lewis (262) set for headliner; Mazany over|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-02-18|access-date=2017-02-19}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox Sports 1)}}\n{{MMAevent [[UFC on Fox: Johnson vs. Reis]]\n}}\n'''''UFC 210: Cormier vs. Johnson 2 ''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on April 8, 2017, at the [[KeyBank Center]] in [[Buffalo, New York]].{{cite news|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/01/ufc-returns-to-buffalo-for-first-time-since-1995-with-ufc-210-on-april-8|title=UFC returns to Buffalo for first time since 1995 with UFC 210 on April 8|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=2017-01-15|access-date=2017-01-15}}\n\n==Background==\n[[File:First Niagara Center front.jpg|thumb|left|240px|[[KeyBank Center]] marked the UFC's first return to [[Buffalo, New York|Buffalo]] in almost 22 years.]]\n\nThe event was the second that the UFC has hosted in Buffalo, the first being [[UFC 7]] in 1995. It was the first to be held in Buffalo since the state of New York lifted its ban on professional mixed martial arts in early 2016.\n\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] rematch between current champion [[Daniel Cormier]] and [[Anthony Johnson (fighter)|Anthony Johnson]] headlined the event.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/1/25/14389808/daniel-cormier-vs-anthony-johnson-2-set-for-ufc-210-in-buffalo|title=Daniel Cormier vs. Anthony Johnson 2 set for UFC 210|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-01-25|access-date=2017-01-25}} The pairing met previously in May 2015 at [[UFC 187]] with Cormier winning the fight (and the vacant title) via submission in the third round.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2015/05/ufc-187-results-daniel-cormier-wears-down-anthony-johnson-for-third-round-tap|title=UFC 187 results: Daniel Cormier wears down Anthony Johnson for third-round tap|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2015-05-24|access-date=2017-01-25}} This rematch was originally scheduled to take place at [[UFC 206]], but Cormier pulled out due to an injury and the bout was scrapped.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2016/11/report-daniel-cormier-injured-forced-out-of-ufc-206-headliner-vs-anthony-johnson|title=Report: Daniel Cormier injured, forced out of UFC 206 headliner vs. Anthony Johnson|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2016-11-25|access-date=2016-11-25}}\n\nControversy arose at the weigh-in as Cormier, who had initially weighed in 1.2 pounds over the 205-pound limit, minutes later weighed in at exactly 205 pounds. Video appeared to show Cormier supporting himself on a towel held by a teammate, though [[New York State Athletic Commission]] (NYSAC) officials denied such allegations.{{Cite web|url=http://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/19102053/daniel-cormier-takes-two-attempts-make-weight-ufc-210-light-heavyweight-title-fight-anthony-johnson|title=Daniel Cormier needs two attempts to make weight for light heavyweight title fight|author=Brett Okamoto|work=espn.com|date=2017-04-07|access-date=2017-04-07}} After this fight, Johnson announced his retirement from the UFC and combat sports, effective immediately.{{Cite web |date=2017-04-10 |title=No longer 'Rumble,' Anthony Johnson posts farewell retirement message following UFC 210 |url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2017/04/no-longer-rumble-anthony-johnson-posts-farewell-retirement-message-following-ufc-210 |access-date=2023-06-03 |website=MMA Junkie |language=en-US}}\n\nPromotional newcomer [[Pearl Gonzalez]] was expected to face [[Cynthia Calvillo]] at the event. She was ruled out of the contest on the day of the weigh ins after New York State Athletic Commission officials confirmed that she could not fight in the state because of her breast implants, which are banned among combat-sports competitors.{{cite web |url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/04/breast-implants-rule-pearl-gonzalez-out-of-saturdays-ufc-210-event|title=Breast implants rule Pearl Gonzalez out of Saturday's UFC 210 event|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2017-04-07|access-date=2017-04-07}} Later the decision was reversed and the fight was allowed to go on as scheduled.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/4/7/15224864/pearl-gonzalez-vs-cynthia-calvillo-fight-back-on-for-ufc-210|title=Pearl Gonzalez vs. Cynthia Calvillo fight back on for UFC 210|author=Marc Raimondi|publisher=mmafighting.com|date=2017-04-07|access-date=2017-04-07}}\n\nDuring the main card broadcast, it was announced that former [[List of WEC champions#Featherweight Championship|WEC Featherweight Champion]] and [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] title challenger [[Urijah Faber]] would be inducted into the Modern Era wing of the [[UFC Hall of Fame]] in this year's ceremony, which took place on July 6 in the midst of the UFC's annual International Fight Week festivities.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/04/urijah-faber-announced-as-first-member-of-2017-ufc-hall-of-fame-class|title=Urijah Faber announced as first member of 2017 UFC Hall of Fame class|author=Staff|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=2017-04-08|access-date=2017-04-08}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Light Heavyweight\n|[[Daniel Cormier]] $50,000 bonuses:{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/?p=241455|title=UFC 210 bonuses: Two undefeated prospects taking home some extra cash|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2017-04-09|date=2017-04-09}}\n*'''Fight of the Night: Shane Burgos vs. Charles Rosa'''\n*'''Performance of the Night:''' '''Charles Oliveira''' and '''Gregor Gillespie'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nThe NYSAC issued a two-month suspension for Oliveira for hopping over the cage after defeating Brooks.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/04/cage-hopping-gets-charles-oliveira-60-day-suspension-from-nysac|title=Cage hopping gets Charles Oliveira 60-day suspension from NYSAC|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2017-04-12|access-date=2017-04-12}}\n\nOn April 13, Johnson's team officially filed an appeal with the NYSAC over the controversial weigh-in of Cormier. In their appeal, they took grievance with the fact that no representative from the NYSAC questioned Cormier over the baffling notion of him losing 1.2 pounds in a matter of minutes. Johnson's team then called for Cormier to address the issue under oath, and if his testimony does not provide a satisfactory explanation, for Cormier to be fined 20 percent of his fight purse and stripped of his UFC light heavyweight title for failing to make weight. Johnson has stated that he does not want Cormier to be stripped of his title but instead be fined 20%.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/4/13/15292820/anthony-johnson-officially-files-appeal-ufc-210-weigh-ins-daniel-cormier-to-be-stripped-of-title|title=Anthony Johnson officially files appeal of UFC 210 weigh-ins, calls for Daniel Cormier to be stripped of title|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|date=2017-04-13|access-date=2017-04-13}} On June 2, the NYSAC denied Johnson's appeal.{{cite web|url=http://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/nysac-denies-retired-ufc-anthony-johnson-appeal|title=NYSAC denies Anthony Johnson's appeal stemming from UFC 210 weigh-in|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2017-06-02|access-date=2017-06-02}}\n\nAt the UFC 2022 Hall of Fame Induction Ceremony, Cormier admitted that he \"may have grabbed the towel\" during the weigh-in, which is believed by many analysts to be a confession.{{cite web |last1=Fuentes |first1=Jon |title=Daniel Cormier Finally Admits To 'Towelgate' During UFC HOF Speech |url=https://www.mmanews.com/2022/07/daniel-cormier-towelgate/ |publisher=mmanews.com |access-date=2022-07-01 |archive-date=2022-07-01 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220701205212/https://www.mmanews.com/2022/07/daniel-cormier-towelgate/ |url-status=dead }}\n\n==See also==\n*[[2017 in UFC]]\n*[[List of UFC Lobov]]\n| followingevent = [[UFC Fight Night: Gustafsson vs. Teixeira]]\n| buyrate =\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 211: Miocic vs. dos Santos 2''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on May 13, 2017, at the [[American Airlines Center]] in Dallas, Texas.\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the fourth that the UFC has hosted in [[Dallas]], following [[UFC 185]] in March 2015, [[UFC 171]] in March 2014, and [[UFC 103]] in September 2009.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/1/24/14371610/ufc-211-headed-for-dallas-will-take-place-may-13|title=UFC 211 headed for Dallas, will take place May 13|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Newswire|date=January 24, 2017|access-date=2017-01-24}}\n\nA [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Stipe Miocic]] and former champion [[Junior dos Santos]] served as the main event.{{cite news|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2017/1/24/14374622/report-stipe-miocic-vs-junior-dos-santos-2-headlines-ufc-211-march-13-dallas|title=Report: Stipe Miocic vs. Junior dos Santos 2 headlines UFC 211 in Dallas|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=January 24, 2017|access-date=2017-01-24}}{{cite news|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/2/6/14522368/stipe-miocic-vs-junior-dos-santos-2-set-for-ufc-211|title=Stipe Miocic vs. Junior dos Santos 2 set for UFC 211|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=February 6, 2017|access-date=2017-02-06}} The pairing met previously in December 2014 at [[UFC on Fox: dos Santos vs. Miocic]], with dos Santos winning by a close unanimous decision.{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Junior-dos-Santos-Denies-Upset-Bid-Decisions-Stipe-Miocic-in-UFC-on-Fox-13-Main-Event-78729|title=Junior dos Santos denies upset bid, decisions Stipe Miocic in UFC on Fox 13 main event|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Brian Knapp|date=December 13, 2014|access-date=2017-02-06}}\n\nIn the co-featured slot, a [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Joanna Jędrzejczyk]] and [[Jéssica Andrade]] took place.{{cite web|url=http://www.mmarocks.pl/joanna-jedrzejczyk/joanna-jedrzejczyk-vs-jessica-andrade-planowane-ufc-211|title=Joanna Jędrzejczyk vs. Jessica Andrade scheduled for UFC 211!|publisher=mmarocks.pl|author=Mariusz Hewelt|date=February 13, 2017|access-date=2017-02-13|language=pl}}{{cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2017/2/13/14600210/ufc-211-joanna-jedrzejczyk-vs-jessica-andrade-set-may-13-dallas-mma|title=UFC 211: Joanna Jedrzejczyk vs Jessica Andrade set for May 13 in Dallas|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=February 13, 2017|access-date=2017-02-13}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between former heavyweight champion [[Fabrício Werdum]] and [[Ben Rothwell]] was originally booked for [[UFC 203]]. However, Rothwell pulled out due to a knee injury.{{cite news|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2016/08/injury-forces-ben-rothwell-out-of-ufc-203-co-main-event-with-fabricio-werdum|title=Injury forces Ben Rothwell out of UFC 203 co-main event with Fabricio Werdum|author=Steven Marrocco|publisher=mmajunkie.com|date=August 11, 2016|access-date=2016-08-11}} The fight was expected to take place at this event.{{cite web|url=http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/2/7/14542494/ufc-211-fabricio-werdum-vs-ben-rothwell-mma-news|title=Fabricio Werdum faces Ben Rothwell at UFC 211|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Lucas Rezende|date=February 7, 2017|access-date=2017-02-07|archive-date=February 9, 2017|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170209071418/http://www.bloodyelbow.com/2017/2/7/14542494/ufc-211-fabricio-werdum-vs-ben-rothwell-mma-news|url-status=dead}} The pairing was scrapped after Rothwell was flagged by USADA for a potential anti-doping violation.{{cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Heavyweight-Ben-Rothwell-Notified-of-Potential-AntiDoping-Violation-118329|title=UFC heavyweight Ben Rothwell notified of potential anti-doping violation|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=March 7, 2017|access-date=2017-03-07}}\n\nA welterweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger [[Demian Maia]] and [[Jorge Masvidal]] was originally targeted to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Swanson vs. Lobov]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/demian-maia-vs-jorge-masvidal-targeted-for-nashvilles-ufc-fight-night-108-headliner|title=Demian Maia vs. Jorge Masvidal targeted for Nashville's UFC Fight Night 108 headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=February 27, 2017|access-date=2017-04-17}} However, in late February, it was announced that the bout was moved to this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/02/demian-maia-vs-jorge-masvidal-set-for-ufc-211-in-dallas|title=Demian Maia vs. Jorge Masvidal set for UFC 211 in Dallas|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=John Morgan|date=February 28, 2017|access-date=2017-04-17}}\n\n[[Jarjis Danho]] was expected to face promotional newcomer Dmitry Poberezhets at the event. However, Danho pulled out of the fight in mid-April citing an injury.{{cite web|url=http://www.rikymma.de/jarjis-danho-sagt-ufc-211-kampf-gegen-dmitry-poberezhets-ab/|title=Jarjis Danho says he is out of UFC 211 fight against Dmitry Poberezhets|publisher=rikymma.de|author=Riky|date=April 14, 2017|access-date=2017-04-17|url-status=dead|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170418000144/http://www.rikymma.de/jarjis-danho-sagt-ufc-211-kampf-gegen-dmitry-poberezhets-ab/|archive-date=April 18, 2017|df=mdy-all|language=de}} He was replaced by [[Chase Sherman]].{{cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/jarjis-danho-out-chase-sherman-in-tegen-dmitry-poberezhets-tijdens-ufc-211|title=Chase Sherman replaces injured Jarjis Danho against Dmitry Poberezhets at UFC 211 in Dallas|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=April 18, 2017|access-date=2017-04-18|language=nl}} In turn, Poberezhets was removed from the card for undisclosed reasons and was replaced by fellow newcomer [[Rashad Coulter]].{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/ufc-211-gets-two-late-fights-added-including-replacement-bringing-card-to-14-total|title=UFC 211 gets two late fights added, including replacement, bringing card to 14 total|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=May 2, 2017|access-date=2017-05-02}}\n\nThe [[Wrestling at the 2008 Summer Olympics – Men's freestyle 55 kg|2008 Olympic gold medalist in freestyle wrestling]] and former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Henry Cejudo]] was expected to face [[Sergio Pettis]] at the event. However, on May 10, it was announced that Cejudo suffered a hand injury and the bout was canceled.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/5/10/15619166/ufc-211s-sergio-pettis-vs-henry-cejudo-fight-canceled|title=UFC 211's Sergio Pettis vs. Henry Cejudo fight canceled|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=May 10, 2017|access-date=2017-05-10}}\n\nA featherweight bout between promotional newcomers, [[Jared Gordon]] and Michel Quiñones was expected for the event. However, Gordon pulled out of the fight on the day before the event due to stomach illness and as a result, Quiñones was removed from the card.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/jared-gordon-ufc-211-with-food-poison-symptoms|title=Jared Gordon off Saturday's UFC 211 card due to 'food poison symptoms'|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|date=May 12, 2017|access-date=2017-05-12}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Heavyweight\n|[[Stipe Miocic]] end|notes=yes}}\n{{cite web |url=https://www.ufc.com/event/ufc-211|title=UFC 211: Miocic vs. dos Santos 2|publisher=Ultimate Fighting Championship|access-date=2017-02-08}}\n\n==Bonus awards==\nThe following fighters were awarded $50,000 bonuses:{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/05/ufc-211-bonuses-nutty-heavyweight-brawl-between-chase-sherman-rashad-coulter-gets-50000|title=UFC 211 bonuses: Nutty heavyweight brawl between Chase Sherman, Rashad Coulter gets $50,000|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Staff|access-date=2017-05-14|date=May 14, 2017}}\n*'''Fight of the Night: Chase Sherman vs. Rashad Coulter'''\n*'''Performance of the Night:''' '''Stipe Miocic''' and '''Jason Knight'''\n\n==Aftermath==\nOn May 26, it was announced that Casey tested positive for elevated levels of [[testosterone]] in an in-competition drug screen on May 13. Per the drug-test results, her testosterone-to-epitestosterone ratio was 5.4:1, above the 4:1 limit. Casey was suspended for three months and her win over Aguilar was overturned to a no-contest by the Texas Department of Licensing and Regulation (TDLR) due to the failure.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/5/26/15701350/cortney-casey-fails-drug-test-at-ufc-211-win-against-jessica-aguilar-overturned-to-no-contest|title=Cortney Casey fails drug test at UFC 211, win against Jessica Aguilar overturned to no-contest|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=May 26, 2017|access-date=2017-05-26}} Nearly a month later, Jeff Novitzky, the UFC's VP of athlete health and performance revealed that Casey's \"B\" sample came back negative for banned substances and synthetic testosterone, therefore clearing her of wrongdoing both by a WADA-accredited lab and a testing laboratory hired by the TDLR. Yet Casey's case remains \"still under review\" by the TDLR. Novitzky urged the TDLR to reverse its decision and cautioned the promotion could steer away future UFC events from Texas if the issue isn't resolved.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.com/2017/06/ufc-anti-doping-cortney-casey-texas-drug-test-wrongdoing|title=Anti-doping chief: UFC may steer away from Texas after Cortney Casey cleared of wrongdoing|publisher=mmajunkie.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=June 23, 2017|access-date=2017-06-24}}\n\nOn June 30, the TDLR lifted Casey's three-month suspension and gave back her victory, which was overturned to a no contest.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2017/6/30/15906772/texas-lifts-sanctions-from-cortney-casey-in-controversial-doping-case|title=Texas lifts sanctions from Cortney Casey in controversial doping case|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=June 30, 2017|access-date=2017-06-30}}\n\n==See also==\n*[[2017 in UFC]]\n*[[List of UFC events]]\n\n==References==\n{{reflist}}\n\n{{UFC Events}}\n{{2017 in mixed martial arts}}\n\n[[Category:Ultimate Fighting Championship flagship events]]\n[[Category:Mixed martial arts events in vs. Johnson 2]]\n| followingevent = [[UFC Fight Night: Swanson vs. Lobov]]\n}}\n'''''UFC on Fox: Johnson vs. Reis''''' (also known as '''''UFC on Fox 24''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] held on April 15, 2017, at the [[Sprint Center]] in [[Kansas City, Missouri]].{{cite news|url=http://cagepages.com/2017/01/27/ufc-kansas-city/|title=UFC expected to hold first event in Kansas City in April|publisher=cagepages.com|author=Farzin Vousoughian|date=2017-01-27|access-date=2017-01-27}}{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/2/3/14499400/a-closer-look-at-the-ufcs-2017-schedule|title=A closer look at the UFC’s 2017 schedule|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2017-02-03|access-date=2017-02-03}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first that the UFC has hosted in the state of [[Missouri]] and the [[Kansas City metropolitan area]]. \n\nA [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] match between current champion [[Demetrious Johnson]] and [[Wilson Reis]] headlined this event.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2017/3/4/14817630/demetrious-johnson-wilson-reis-jacare-souza-robert-whittaker-ufc-on-fox-24|title=Demetrious Johnson vs. Wilson Reis, ‘Jacare' Souza vs. Robert Whittaker set for UFC on FOX 24|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2017-03-04|access-date=2017-03-05}} The pairing was originally booked for [[UFC 201]], but Johnson pulled out due to an undisclosed injury and the bout was scrapped.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmafighting.com/2016/7/8/12132078/demetrious-johnson-injured-fight-with-wilson-reis-off-of-ufc-201|title=Demetrious Johnson injured, out of UFC 201 title fight with Wilson Reis|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2016-07-08|access-date=2017-03-05}}\n\nUFC officials were initially targeting a featherweight bout between former [[UFC Bantamweight Championship|UFC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Renan Barão]] and [[Choi Doo-ho]] to serve as the event headliner.{{cite news|url=http://www.mmamad.com/2017/02/12/renan-barao-vs-doo-ho-choi-headlines-ufc-on-fox-24-in-kansas-city-missouri/|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20170213164026/http://www.mmamad.com/2017/02/12/renan-barao-vs-doo-ho-choi-headlines-ufc-on-fox-24-in-kansas-city-missouri/|url-status=usurped|archive-date=February 13, 2017|title=Renan Barao vs. Doo Ho Choi headlines UFC on FOX 24 in Kansas City, Missouri|publisher=mmamad.com|author=Dale Jordan|date=2017-02-12|access-date=2017-02-12}} However, as the announcement of the pairing began to circulate, Choi declined the bout, and as a result, Barao was rescheduled against a different opponent at another event.{{cite news|url=http://www.flocombat.com/article/51907-why-doo-ho-choi-versus-renan-barao-isn-t-happening#.WKD15G8rLIU|title=Why Doo Ho Choi versus Renan Barao isn't happening|publisher=mmamad.com|author=Jim Edwards|date=2017-02-12|access-date=2017-02-12}}\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main Card (Fox)}}\n{{MMAevent bout\n|Flyweight\n|[[Demetrious Johnson]] (c)\n|def.\n|[[Wilson
